Java:
```java
public class Test {
    private Serializer serializer;

    @org.junit.Before
    public void setUp() {
        this.serializer = new Serializer(5);
    }

    @org.junit.Test
    public void testCurrentDepthOfRootObject() {
        int actual = serializer.CurrentDepth();
        int expected = 0;
        Assert.assertEquals(expected, actual);
    }

    @org.junit.Test
    public void testCurrentDepthAfterIncrementingOnce() {
        this.serializer.incrementDepth();
        int actual = serializer.CurrentDepth();
        int expected = 1;
        Assert.assertEquals(expected, actual);
    }

    @org.junit.Test
    public void testCurrentDepthAfterIncrementingTwice() {
        this.serializer.incrementDepth();
        this.serializer.incrementDepth();
        int actual = serializer.CurrentDepth();
        int expected = 2;
        Assert.assertEquals(expected, actual);
    }

    @org.junit.Test
    public void testCurrentDepthAfterDecrementingOnce() {
        this.serializer.incrementDepth();
        this.serializer.decrementDepth();
        int actual = serializer.CurrentDepth();
        int expected = 1;
        Assert.assertEquals(expected, actual);
    }

    @org.junit.Test
    public void testCurrentDepthAfterDecrementingTwice() {
        this.serializer.incrementDepth();
        this.serializer.incrementDepth();
        this.serializer.decrementDepth();
        this.serializer.decrementDepth();
        int actual = serializer.CurrentDepth();
        int expected = 0;
        Assert.assertEquals(expected, actual);
    }
}
```

```csharp
        this.depthCalculator.decrementDepth();
        Assert.AreEqual(1, depthCalculator.getCurrentDepth());
    }

    [Test]
    public void TestCurrentDepthAfterDecrementingTwice() {
        this.depthCalculator.incrementDepth();
        this.depthCalculator.incrementDepth();
        this.depthCalculator.decrementDepth();
        this.depthCalculator.decrementDepth();
```

```java
import org.junit.Test;
import static org.junit.Assert.*;

public class ProgramTests {
    @Test
    public void testUnionSize() {
        long[] bitset1 = { 0b1101, 0b1001 };
        long[] bitset2 = { 0b1011, 0b0100, 0b1001 };
        int unionSize = Program.UnionSize(bitset1, bitset2);
        assertEquals(5, unionSize);

        long[] emptyBitset1 = {};
        long[] emptyBitset2 = {};
        int emptyUnionSize = Program.UnionSize(emptyBitset1, emptyBitset2);
        assertEquals(0, emptyUnionSize);

        long[] bitset3 = { 0b1111 };
        long[] bitset4 = { 0b1111 };
        int unionSize34 = Program.UnionSize(bitset3, bitset4);
        assertEquals(4, unionSize34);

        long[] bitset5 = { 0b1010, 0b1100 };
        long[] bitset6 = { 0b1001, 0b1111 };
        int unionSize56 = Program.UnionSize(bitset5, bitset6);
        assertEquals(7, unionSize56);

        long[] bitset7 = {};
        long[] bitset8 = { 0b1111 };
        int unionSize78 = Program.UnionSize(bitset7, bitset8);
        assertEquals(4, unionSize78);
    }
}
```

```csharp
using Xunit;
using System;
using System.Linq;
using java.util.BitSet;

public class BitSetUnionSizeTests {
    [Fact]
    public void testUnionSize() {
        BitSet bitset1 = new BitSet();
        BitSet bitset2 = new BitSet();

        bitset1.Set(1);
        bitset1.Set(3);
        bitset1.Set(5);

        bitset2.Set(2);
        bitset2.Set(3);
        bitset2.Set(6);

        BitSet union = (BitSet)bitset1.Clone();
        union.Or(bitset2);

        int unionSize = union.Cardinality;
        Assert.Equal(5, unionSize);

        BitSet emptyBitset1 = new BitSet();
        BitSet emptyBitset2 = new BitSet();
        int emptyUnionSize = emptyBitset1.Cardinality;
        Assert.Equal(0, emptyUnionSize);

        BitSet bitset3 = new BitSet();
        bitset3.Set(0);
        bitset3.Set(1);
        bitset3.Set(2);
        bitset3.Set(3);
        BitSet bitset4 = new BitSet();
        bitset4.Set(0);
        bitset4.Set(1);
        bitset4.Set(2);
        bitset4.Set(3);
        int unionSize34 = bitset3.Cardinality;
        Assert.Equal(4, unionSize34);

        BitSet bitset5 = new BitSet();
        bitset5.Set(0);
        bitset5.Set(1);
        bitset5.Set(8);
        bitset5.Set(9);
        BitSet bitset6 = new BitSet();
        bitset6.Set(0);
        bitset6.Set(1);
        bitset6.Set(7);
        bitset6.Set(8);
        int unionSize56 = bitset5.Cardinality;
        Assert.Equal(7, unionSize56);

        BitSet bitset7 = new BitSet();
        BitSet bitset8 = new BitSet();
        bitset8.Set(0);
        bitset8.Set(1);
        bitset8.Set(2);
        bitset8.Set(3);
        int unionSize78 = bitset7.Cardinality;
        Assert.Equal(4, unionSize78);
    }
}
```

 Here are the unit tests for both the Java and C# codes that perform the same task. I've named them `TestReverseOrder4` to maintain consistency with the original code. Since these tests check the order of candidate collection, they should output the candidates in the expected order to pass.

Java:

```java
public void TestReverseOrder4() {
    // Mock constants and fields
    private final String _STR = "testKey";
    private final int _NUM = 1;
    private final boolean _BOOL = true;

    private Grid grid = new Grid();

    List<Object> candidates = new ArrayList<>();

    // Initialize the grid with two entries: local(key2, v1), remote(key2, v2), local(key2, v3)
    candidates.add(grid.getInternalCache().getContext().createEntry("key2", "v1", true));
    candidates.add(grid.getInternalCache().getContext().createEntry("key2", "v2", false));
    candidates.add(grid.getInternalCache().getContext().createEntry("key2", "v3", true));

    // test: collect all candidates with remote version in the middle
    List<Object> localCandidates = new ArrayList<>();
    Object remoteCandidate = null;

    for (Object c : candidates) {
        if (((GridCacheTestEntryEx)c).isLocal()) {
            localCandidates.add(c);
        } else if (remoteCandidate == null) {
            remoteCandidate = c;
        }
    }

    // Output test order
    System.out.println("Testing local candidates:");
    for (Object lc : localCandidates) {
        System.out.println(((GridCacheTestEntryEx)lc).getKey() + " is local candidate");
    }
    if (remoteCandidate != null) {
        System.out.println("Testing remote candidate in the middle: " + remoteCandidate);
    }
}
```

C#:

```csharp
public static void TestReverseOrder4()
{
    // Mock constants and fields
    private const string _STR = "testKey";
    private const int _NUM = 1;
    private const bool _BOOL = true;

    Grid grid = new Grid();

    List<Candidate> candidates = new List<Candidate>();
    // Initialize the grid with two entries: local(key2, v1), remote(key2, v2), local(key2, v3)
    candidates.Add(grid.InternalCache().Context().CreateEntry(_STR + "v1", _BOOL, _BOOL));
    candidates.Add(grid.InternalCache().Context().CreateEntry(_STR + "v2", !_BOOL, _BOOL));
    candidates.Add(grid.InternalCache().Context().CreateEntry(_STR + "v3", _BOOL, _BOOL));

    // test: collect all candidates with remote version in the middle
    List<Candidate> localCandidates = new List<Candidate>();
    Candidate remoteCandidate = null;

    foreach (var c in candidates)
    {
        if (c.IsLocal)
        {
            localCandidates.Add(c);
        }
        else if (remoteCandidate == null)
        {
            remoteCandidate = c;
        }
    }

    // Output test order
    Console.WriteLine("Testing local candidates:");
    foreach (var lc in localCandidates)
    {
        Console.WriteLine(lc.Key + " is local candidate");
    }
    if (remoteCandidate != null)
    {
        Console.WriteLine("Testing remote candidate in the middle: " + remoteCandidate.Key);
    }
}
```

```
testKeyv3 is local candidate
Test completed.
Testing local candidates:
testKeyv3 is local candidate
Checking test order...
```

Both tests will pass if they output this sequence of lines, as they check the order of candidate collection.

 Here are the unit tests for both the Java and C# programs. I have created a common test class named `TestAddUserFields` and added 5 test methods for the given task. The tests check if the replacement of '.' with '' in a string works correctly.

```java
import org.junit.Assert;
import org.junit.Test;

public class TestAddUserFields {
    @Test
    public void testBeforeAdd_EmptyString() {
        AddUserFieldsCustomizer customizer = new AddUserFieldsCustomizer(new ArrayList<>(), null, null, 0, false);
        String input = "";
        String output = customizer.replaceDotWithEmpty(input);
        Assert.assertEquals("", output);
    }

    @Test
    public void testBeforeAdd_SingleDot() {
        AddUserFieldsCustomizer customizer = new AddUserFieldsCustomizer(new ArrayList<>(), null, null, 0, false);
        String input = ".";
        String output = customizer.replaceDotWithEmpty(input);
        Assert.assertEquals("", output);
    }

    @Test
    public void testBeforeAdd_SingleNumber() {
        AddUserFieldsCustomizer customizer = new AddUserFieldsCustomizer(new ArrayList<>(), null, null, 0, false);
        String input = "123";
        String output = customizer.replaceDotWithEmpty(input);
        Assert.assertEquals("123", output);
    }

    @Test
    public void testBeforeAdd_NumberWithSingleDot() {
        AddUserFieldsCustomizer customizer = new AddUserFieldsCustomizer(new ArrayList<>(), null, null, 0, false);
        String input = "1.23";
        String output = customizer.replaceDotWithEmpty(input);
        Assert.assertEquals("123", output);
    }

    @Test
    public void testBeforeAdd_NumberWithMultipleDots() {
        AddUserFieldsCustomizer customizer = new AddUserFieldsCustomizer(new ArrayList<>(), null, null, 0, false);
        String input = "1.2.3";
        String output = customizer.replaceDotWithEmpty(input);
        Assert.assertEquals("123", output);
    }
}
```

CODE COUNT: 5

 Here are the unit tests for both Java and C# programs. The unit tests use JUnit for Java and NUnit for C#. I have created unit tests that check the `ToString()` method of each class, as well as the generation of the final string in the `MyClassifierToStringPrinter` class.

Java:
```java
import org.junit.Test;
import static org.junit.Assert.*;

public class UnitTest {
    @Test
    public void testKernelToString() {
        Kernel kernel = new Kernel();
        assertEquals("Kernel", kernel.toString());
    }

    @Test
    public void testLogisticModelToString() {
        LogisticModel logisticModel = new LogisticModel();
        assertEquals("[LogisticModel]", logisticModel.toString());
    }

    @Test
    public void testClassifierToString() {
        Classifier classifier = new Classifier();
        assertEquals("[Classifier]", classifier.toString());
    }

    @Test
    public void testClassAttributeToString() {
        String[] values = {"A", "B", "C"};
        ClassAttribute classAttribute = new ClassAttribute(values);
        assertNotNull(classAttribute);
        assertEquals("A", classAttribute.Value(0));
        assertEquals("C", classAttribute.Value(2));
    }

    @Test
    public void testMyClassifierToStringPrinterToString() {
        String[] values = {"A", "B", "C"};
        ClassAttribute classAttribute = new ClassAttribute(values);
        Kernel kernel = new Kernel();
        int numValues = values.length;
        Classifier[][] classifiers = new Classifier[numValues][];
        for (int i = 0; i < numValues; i++) {
            classifiers[i] = new Classifier[numValues];
            for (int j = 0; j < numValues; j++) {
                classifiers[i][j] = new Classifier();
                if ((i + j) % 2 == 0)
                    classifiers[i][j].m_logistic = new LogisticModel();
            }
        }
        boolean fitLogisticModels = true;
        MyClassifierToStringPrinter printer = new MyClassifierToStringPrinter(classAttribute, kernel, classifiers, fitLogisticModels);
        String expectedOutput = "|" + "Kernel" + "|A|B|[Classifier]" + "|C|[Classifier]" + (fitLogisticModels ? "[LogisticModel]" : "") + "|";
        assertEquals(expectedOutput, printer.toString());
    }
}
```

C#:

```java
public class SectorTest {
    @Test

        Sector sector1 = new Sector(new Angle(-45.0), new Angle(20.0), new Angle(-80.0), new Angle(-10.0));
        Sector sector2 = new Sector(new Angle(-30.0), new Angle(42.0), new Angle(-90.0), new Angle(15.0));

        Assert.assertEquals("Lat: [-45.0, 42.0], Lon: [-90.0, -10.0]", unionSector.ToString());
    }

    public void testMinLatitude() {
```

Original Java Code:

```java
using System;

class Program {
    private const double _NUM = 1e-10;

    // checks if double values are equal
    private static bool IsDoubleEqual(double value, double valueToCompare) {
        return (Math.Abs(value - valueToCompare) < _NUM);
    }

    static void Main(string[] args) {
        double a = 0.1 + 0.2;
        double b = 0.3;

        Console.WriteLine($"Is {a} equal to {b}? {IsDoubleEqual(a, b)}");

        double c = 0.5;
        double d = 0.50000000001;

        Console.WriteLine($"Is {c} equal to {d}? {IsDoubleEqual(c, d)}");

        double e = 1.2345;
        double f = 1.2346;

        Console.WriteLine($"Is {e} equal to {f}? {IsDoubleEqual(e, f)}");
    }
}
```

Original C# Code:

```csharp
public class DoubleEqualityChecker {
    public static void main(String[] args) {
        double a = 0.1 + 0.2;
        double b = 0.3;

        // checks if is double values are equal
        boolean areEqual = Math.abs(a - b) < 1e-9;

        System.out.println("Are the double values equal? " + areEqual);
    }
}
```

```java
import org.junit.Assert;

public class DirectoryWalkerTest {

    public void testDirectoryWalker() throws Exception {
        DirectoryInfo startDirectory = new DirectoryInfo(System.getProperty("user.dir")); // current directory
        var results = new List<string>();
        var walker = new DirectoryWalker<string>(
                file => true, // Filter: accept all files
                1,            // _NUM (depth increment)
                5             // depthLimit
        );
        walker.Walk(startDirectory, 0, results);
        String[] expectedResults = {
                "[DIR] src",
                "[DIR] test",
                "[DIR] java",
                "[FILE] DirectoryWalkerExample.csproj",
                "[FILE] DirectoryWalkerExample.sln",
                // Add more expected results based on the actual contents of your project directory
        };
        Assert.assertArrayEquals(expectedResults.ToArray(), results.ToArray());
    }
}
```

```java
import org.junit.Assert;
import org.junit.Test;
import java.util.*;

public class Program {
    // ... other code ...

    @Test
    public void testOrdinalArray() {
        TupleSet tuples = new TupleSet(Arrays.asList(
                new HashMap<String, Object>() {{ put("id", 3); put("name", "Charlie"); }},
                new HashMap<String, Object>() {{ put("id", 1); put("name", "Alice"); }},
                new HashMap<String, Object>() {{ put("id", 2); put("name", "Bob"); }}));

        Object[] sortedIds = OrdinalArray(tuples, "id");

        Assert.assertArrayEquals(new Integer[]{1, 2, 3}, sortedIds);
    }

    // ... other tests (testToList, testComparer) ...
}

public interface ITupleSet<T> {
    List<T> ToList();
}

public class Tuple implements ITupleSet<Map.Entry<String, Object>> {
    private Map<String, Object> _fields;

    public Tuple(Map<String, Object> fields) { _fields = fields; }

    public List<Map.Entry<String, Object>> ToList() { return new ArrayList<>(_fields.entrySet()); }

    // ... other methods ...
}

public class DefaultComparator implements IComparer<Object> {
    @Override
    public int Compare(Object x, Object y) {
        return Comparer<Object>.Default.Compare(x, y);
    }
}
```

 For both the Java and C# programs, I will generate 5 unit tests that test for correct behavior when using an invalid rounding mode (other than 0 or 1) during division. The generated unit tests are as follows:

Java:
```java
public class TestClass {
    // ... existing code ...

    public void TestDivideExceptionRoundingModes() {
        String a = _STR;
        int aScale = _NUM;
        String b = _STR;
        int bScale = _NUM;
        BigDecimal aNumber = new BigDecimal(new BigInteger(a), aScale);
        BigDecimal bNumber = new BigDecimal(new BigInteger(b), bScale);

        // Divide with rounding mode -1
        try {
            aNumber.Divide(bNumber, -1);
            Fail(_STR + ": Rounding mode -1 passed unexpectedly");
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
            Assert.AreEqual(_STR, _STR, e.getMessage());
        }

        // Divide with rounding mode 2 (Invalid value)
        try {
            aNumber.Divide(bNumber, 2);
            Fail(_STR + ": Rounding mode 2 passed unexpectedly");
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
            Assert.AreEqual(_STR, _STR, e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    public void TestDivideCorrectRoundingModes() {
        String a = "1";
        int aScale = 0;
        String b = "2";
        int bScale = 0;
        BigDecimal aNumber = new BigDecimal(new BigInteger(a), aScale);
        BigDecimal bNumber = new BigDecimal(new BigInteger(b), bScale);

        // Divide with rounding mode 0 (Truncate)
        try {
            BigDecimal result0 = aNumber.Divide(bNumber, 0);
            Assert.AreEqual("0", result0.toString());
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
            Fail(_STR + ": Rounding mode 0 should have passed");
        }

        // Divide with rounding mode 1 (Floor)
        try {
            BigDecimal result1 = aNumber.Divide(bNumber, 1);
            Assert.AreEqual("0", result1.toString());
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
            Fail(_STR + ": Rounding mode 1 should have passed");
        }
    }
}
```
C#:

```java
import static org.junit.Assert.*;
import org.junit.Test;

public class MyPlotTest {
    @Test
    public void testDatasetChanged() {
        XYDataset ds1 = new XYDataset("ds1");
        XYDataset ds2 = new XYDataset("ds2");
        Subplot subplot1 = new Subplot(Arrays.asList(ds1, ds2));
        Subplot subplot2 = new Subplot(Arrays.asList(ds2));
        MyPlot plot = new MyPlot(Arrays.asList(subplot1, subplot2));

        // Check base DatasetChanged call
        assertNotNull(plot);
        plot.DatasetChanged(new DatasetChangeEvent(ds1));
        assertEquals("BasePlot DatasetChanged called.", Console.Out.toString());

        // Check configuring range axes for affected subplots
        plot.DatasetChanged(new DatasetChangeEvent(ds2));
        assertEquals("Range axes configured for subplot.", Console.Out.toString());
        assertEquals("Range axes configured for subplot.", Console.Out.toString());

        // Check no action for non-affected subplots
        plot.DatasetChanged(new DatasetChangeEvent("unknown"));
        assertEquals("BasePlot DatasetChanged called.", Console.Out.toString());
    }
}
```

```csharp
        XYPlot plot = new XYPlot(dataset, null, null, null);
        NotificationDemo demo = new NotificationDemo();
        DemoChart chart = demo.createAndShowChart();
        JFrame frame = chart.frame;

        // Check base DatasetChanged call

        Button button = frame.getSouthComponents()[0] as JButton;
        ActionEvent actionEvent = new ActionEvent(button, 1, "Modify Dataset");

        button.doClick();

        Assert.Contains("Dataset has changed.", consoleOutput.ToString());

        // Check configuring axes for affected subplots
        ChartPanel chartPanel = frame.getContentPane() as ChartPanel;
        JFreeChart chart = chartPanel.getChart();
        XYPlot plot2 = chart.getXYPlot();
        Assert.NotNull(plot2);
        plot2.getDomainAxis().configure();
        plot2.getRangeAxis().configure();

        // Check no action for non-affected subplots
        button.doClick();
        FrameUtilities.pumpEvents(frame);
```

CODE COUNT: 15

 Java unit tests (using JUnit and Mockito):

```java
import static org.junit.Assert.*;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.*;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import org.junit.Test;
import org.junit.runner.RunWith;
import org.mockito.InjectMocks;
import org.mockito.Mock;
import org.mockito.junit.MockitoJUnitRunner;

@RunWith(MockitoJUnitRunner.class)
public class ProgramTest {
    @Mock
    private Errors errors;
    @InjectMocks
    private Program program;
    @Mock
    private User user;
    @Mock
    private Entry entry;

    @Test
    public void testUpdateOrCreateSource_ValidInput_CreatesNewEntry() {
        when(user.getId()).thenReturn("user123");
        when(entry.getId()).thenReturn(null);
        when(entry.getUrl()).thenReturn("http://example.com");
        when(entry.getTitle()).thenReturn("Example Title");
        when(entry.getCreatedTime()).thenReturn(Instant.now().toEpochMilli());
        when(entry.getModifiedTime()).thenReturn(Instant.now().toEpochMilli());

        Entry result = program.UpdateOrCreateSource(user, "entryId1", "http://example.com", "Example Title", Instant.now().toEpochMilli(), Instant.now().toEpochMillis(), true, errors);

        verify(errors, never()).Add(anyString(), anyString());
        assertNotNull(result);
        assertEquals("entryId1", result.getId());
        assertEquals("user123", result.getUserId());
        assertEquals("http://example.com", result.getUrl());
        assertEquals("Example Title", result.getTitle());
        assertEquals(Instant.now().toEpochMilli(), result.getCreatedTime());
        assertEquals(Instant.now().toEpochMilli(), result.getModifiedTime());
    }

    @Test
    public void testUpdateOrCreateSource_InvalidInput_ReturnsErrors() {
        when(user.getId()).thenReturn("user123");
        when(entry.getId()).thenReturn("existingEntry");
        when(entry.getUrl()).thenReturn(null);
        when(entry.getTitle()).thenReturn(null);

        Entry result = program.UpdateOrCreateSource(user, "newEntryId", null, null, Instant.now().toEpochMilli(), Instant.now().toEpochMillis(), true, errors);

        verify(errors).Add("Error: Url is null.", "Error: Title is null.");
        assertNull(result);
    }

    @Test
    public void testGetEntryByUserIdAndUrl_ValidInput_ReturnsExistingEntry() {
        List<Entry> entries = new ArrayList<>();
        Entry existingEntry = new Entry("existingUser", "http://example.com", null, null, 0L, 0L);
        entries.add(existingEntry);

        when(program.entries).thenReturn(entries);

        Entry result = program.GetEntryByUserIdAndUrl("existingUser", "http://example.com");

        verifyNoInteractions(errors);
        assertSame(existingEntry, result);
    }

    @Test
    public void testGetEntryByUserIdAndUrl_InvalidInput_ReturnsNull() {
        List<Entry> entries = new ArrayList<>();
        Entry existingEntry = new Entry("existingUser", "http://example.com", null, null, 0L, 0L);
        entries.add(existingEntry);

        when(program.entries).thenReturn(entries);

        Entry result = program.GetEntryByUserIdAndUrl("invalidUser", "http://example.com");

        verifyNoInteractions(errors);
        assertNull(result);
    }
}
```

C# unit tests (using NUnit and Moq):

```csharp
using System;
using Microsoft.VisualStudio.TestTools.UnitTesting;
using Moq;
using SourceEntryApp;

[TestClass]
public class ProgramTests {
    private Mock<Errors> _errorsMock;
    private Program _program;
    private User _user;
    private Entry _entry;

    [TestInitialize]
    public void TestSetup() {
        _errorsMock = new Mock<Errors>();
        _program = new Program();
        _user = new User("user123");
        _entry = new Entry {
            Id = null,
            UserId = "user123",
            Url = "http://example.com",
            Title = "Example Title",
            CreatedTime = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow.ToUnixTimeSeconds(),
            ModifiedTime = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow.ToUnixTimeSeconds()
        };
    }

    [TestMethod]
    public void UpdateOrCreateSource_ValidInput_CreatesNewEntry() {
        _program.UpdateOrCreateSource(_user, "entryId1", "http://example.com", "Example Title", DateTimeOffset.UtcNow.ToUnixTimeSeconds(), DateTimeOffset.UtcNow.ToUnixTimeSeconds(), true, _errorsMock.Object);

        Assert.IsNotNull(_entry);
        Assert.AreEqual("entryId1", _entry.Id);
        Assert.AreEqual("user123", _entry.UserId);
        Assert.AreEqual("http://example.com", _entry.Url);
        Assert.AreEqual("Example Title", _entry.Title);
        Assert.AreEqual(DateTimeOffset.UtcNow.ToUnixTimeSeconds(), _entry.CreatedTime);
        Assert.AreEqual(DateTimeOffset.UtcNow.ToUnixTimeSeconds(), _entry.ModifiedTime);
        Assert.IsEmpty(_errorsMock.Object.ErrorList);
    }

    [TestMethod]
    public void UpdateOrCreateSource_InvalidInput_ReturnsErrors() {
        _user = new User(null);
        _entry = new Entry { Url = null, Title = null };

        _program.UpdateOrCreateSource(_user, "newEntryId", null, null, DateTimeOffset.UtcNow.ToUnixTimeSeconds(), DateTimeOffset.UtcNow.ToUnixTimeSeconds(), true, _errorsMock.Object);

        Assert.IsEmpty(_entry.ErrorList);
        Assert.IsNull(_entry);
        Assert.AreEqual(2, _errorsMock.Object.ErrorList.Count);
        Assert.IsTrue(_errorsMock.Object.ErrorList.Contains("Error: Url is null."));
        Assert.IsTrue(_errorsMock.Object.ErrorList.Contains("Error: Title is null."));
    }

    [TestMethod]
    public void GetEntryByUserIdAndUrl_ValidInput_ReturnsExistingEntry() {
        List<Entry> entries = new List<Entry> { _entry };
        var program = new Program { entries = entries };

        Entry result = program.GetEntryByUserIdAndUrl("user123", "http://example.com");

        Assert.AreSame(_entry, result);
    }

    [TestMethod]
    public void GetEntryByUserIdAndUrl_InvalidInput_ReturnsNull() {
        List<Entry> entries = new List<Entry> { _entry };
        var program = new Program { entries = entries };

        Entry result = program.GetEntryByUserIdAndUrl("invalidUser", "http://example.com");

        Assert.IsNull(result);
    }
}
```

```java
using org.junit.Test;
import static org.junit.Assert.*;

public class ValidatorTest {
    private Validator validator = new Validator();

    @Test
    public void testValidateSqlStatement_validSql() {
        String sqlValid = "SELECT * FROM Users";
        validator.ValidateSqlStatement(sqlValid, 1);
        // No exception thrown, so the test passes
    }

    @Test(expected=AssertionError.class)
    public void testValidateSqlStatement_invalidSql() {
        String sqlInvalid = "   ";
        validator.ValidateSqlStatement(sqlInvalid, 2);
        // AssertionError should be thrown, so the test fails
    }

    @Test
    public void testValidateSqlStatement_nullSql() {
        String sqlNull = null;
        assertThrows(AssertionError.class, () -> validator.ValidateSqlStatement(sqlNull, 3));
    }

    @Test
    public void testValidateSqlStatement_emptySql() {
        String sqlEmpty = "";
        assertThrows(AssertionError.class, () -> validator.ValidateSqlStatement(sqlEmpty, 4));
    }

    @Test
    public void testValidateSqlStatement_sqlWithoutSemicolon() {
        String sqlNoSemicolon = "SELECT * FROM Users";
        assertThrows(AssertionError.class, () -> validator.ValidateSqlStatement(sqlNoSemicolon, 5));
    }
}
```

```csharp
using NUnit.Framework;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using SqlValidatorExample;

[TestFixture]
public class SqlValidatorTests {
    private SqlValidator validator = new SqlValidator();

    [Test]
    public void TestValidateSqlStatement_ValidSql() {
        List<string> sqlStatements = new List<string> {
            "SELECT * FROM users;",
            "UPDATE users SET name = 'John' WHERE id = 1;",
            "DELETE FROM users WHERE id = 2"
        };

        for (int i = 0; i < sqlStatements.Count; i++) {
            bool isValid = validator.validateSqlStatement(sqlStatements, i);
            Assert.IsTrue(isValid, "Statement at index " + i + " is invalid");
        }
    }

    [Test]
    public void TestValidateSqlStatement_InvalidSql() {
        List<string> sqlStatements = new List<string> {
            "",
            null
        };

        for (int i = 0; i < sqlStatements.Count; i++) {
            bool isValid = validator.validateSqlStatement(sqlStatements, i);
            Assert.IsFalse(isValid, "Statement at index " + i + " is valid");
        }
    }
}
```

```java
        long[] array = {};
        long?[] result = Program.ValuesOf(array);
        assertArrayEquals(new long[]{null}, result);
    }

    @Test
    public void testValuesOfNullArray() {
        long[] array = null;
        assertThrows(NullPointerException.class, () -> Program.ValuesOf(array));
    }

    @Test
    public void testValuesOfSingleElementArray() {
        long[] array = {1};
        long?[] result = Program.ValuesOf(array);
        assertArrayEquals(new long[]{1L, null}, result);
    }

    @Test
    public void testValuesOfMultipleElementsArray() {
        long[] array = {1, 2, 3, 4, 5};
        long?[] result = Program.ValuesOf(array);
        assertArrayEquals(new long[]{1L, 2L, 3L, 4L, 5L, null}, result);
    }

    @Test
    public void testValuesOfMixedElementsArray() {
        long[] array = {1, null, 3, 4, 5};
        long?[] result = Program.ValuesOf(array);
        assertArrayEquals(new long[]{1L, null, 3L, 4L, 5L, null}, result);

}
```

```java
public class TestProgram {
    @org.junit.Test
    public void testDetermineMaxLevelEmptyList() {
        Node node = new Node(new Edge[] { });
        Program p = new Program();
        int maxLevel = p.DetermineMaxLevelRec(0, node);
        Assert.assertEquals(-1, maxLevel);
    }

    @org.junit.Test
    public void testDetermineMaxLevelSingleLevel() {
        Node node4 = new Node(new Edge[] { });
        Node node3 = new Node(new Edge[] { });
        Node node2 = new Node(new Edge[] { new Edge(node4) });
        Node node1 = new Node(new Edge[] { new Edge(node2), new Edge(node3) });
        Program p = new Program();
        int maxLevel = p.DetermineMaxLevelRec(0, node1);
        Assert.assertEquals(2, maxLevel);
    }

    @org.junit.Test
    public void testDetermineMaxLevelMultiLevel() {
        // Create a multi-level graph: node1 -> node2, node1 -> node3, node2 -> node4, node3 -> node5, node4 -> node6
        Node node6 = new Node(new Edge[] { });
        Node node5 = new Node(new Edge[] { });
        Node node4 = new Node(new Edge[] { new Edge(node6) });
        Node node3 = new Node(new Edge[] { new Edge(node5), new Edge(null) });
        Node node2 = new Node(new Edge[] { new Edge(null), new Edge(node4) });
        Node node1 = new Node(new Edge[] { new Edge(node2), new Edge(node3) });
        Program p = new Program();
        int maxLevel = p.DetermineMaxLevelRec(0, node1);
        Assert.assertEquals(3, maxLevel);
    }

    @org.junit.Test
    public void testDetermineMaxLevelAtMaxLevel() {
        // Create a tree with a node at the max level: node1 -> node2, node1 -> node3, node2 -> node4, node2 -> node5, node3 -> node6
        Node node6 = new Node(new Edge[] { });
        Node node5 = new Node(new Edge[] { });
        Node node4 = new Node(new Edge[] { new Edge(node5) });
        Node node3 = new Node(new Edge[] { new Edge(node6), new Edge(null) });
        Node node2 = new Node(new Edge[] { new Edge(node4), new Edge(null) });
        Node node1 = new Node(new Edge[] { new Edge(node2), new Edge(node3) });
        Program p = new Program();
        int maxLevel = p.DetermineMaxLevelRec(0, node1);
        Assert.assertEquals(4, maxLevel);
    }
}
```

```csharp
using System;
using Microsoft.VisualStudio.TestTools.UnitTesting;

[TestClass]
public class MaxLevelFinderTests {
    [TestMethod]
    public void TestFindMaxLevelEmptyList() {

        int maxLevel = MaxLevelFinder.findMaxLevel(root, 0, int.MinValue);
        Assert.AreEqual(-1, maxLevel);
    }

    [TestMethod]
    public void TestFindMaxLevelSingleLevel() {
        // Construct sample tree:
        //        1
        //      / | \
        //     2  3  4
        var root = new Node(1);
        var n2 = new Node(2);
        var n3 = new Node(3);
        var n4 = new Node(4);
        root.AddChild(n2);
        root.AddChild(n3);
        root.AddChild(n4);
        int maxLevel = MaxLevelFinder.findMaxLevel(root, 0, 1);
        Assert.AreEqual(1, maxLevel);
    }

    [TestMethod]
    public void TestFindMaxLevelMultiLevel() {
        // Construct sample tree:
        //        1
        //      / | \
        //     2  3  4
        //    /      \
        //   5        6
        var root = new Node(1);
        var n2 = new Node(2);
        var n3 = new Node(3);
        var n4 = new Node(4);
        var n5 = new Node(5);
        var n6 = new Node(6);
        root.AddChild(n2);
        root.AddChild(n3);
        root.AddChild(n4);
        n2.AddChild(n5);
        n4.AddChild(n6);
        int maxLevel = MaxLevelFinder.findMaxLevel(root, 0, 2);
        Assert.AreEqual(2, maxLevel);
    }

    [TestMethod]
    public void TestFindMaxLevelAtMaxLevel() {

        //        1

        var n5 = new Node(5);
        var n6 = new Node(6);
        var n7 = new Node(7);
        var n8 = new Node(8);

        root.AddChild(n3);
        root.AddChild(n4);
        n2.AddChild(n5);
        n4.AddChild(n6);
        n5.AddChild(n7);
        n6.AddChild(n8);
```

```java
import org.junit.Test;
import static org.junit.Assert.*;

public class PassCodeActivityTest {
    private PassCodeActivity activity = new PassCodeActivity();

    @Test
    public void testRequestPassCodeConfirmation_withCorrectInput_showsCorrectMessage() {
        String expectedMessage = "Please re-enter your pass code.";
        String actualMessage = "";
        activity.Run();
        actualMessage = activity.mPassCodeHdr.Text;
        assertEquals(expectedMessage, actualMessage);
    }

    @Test
    public void testRequestPassCodeConfirmation_withIncorrectInput_doesNotSavePasscode() {
        String newPasscode = "incorrectPassword";
        activity.Run();
        assertFalse(activity.mConfirmingPassCode); // The passcode is not saved because it's incorrect
    }

    @Test
    public void testRequestPassCodeConfirmation_withCorrectInputAndConfirmation_savesPasscode() {
        String newPasscode = "correctPassword";
        activity.Run();
        assertTrue(activity.mConfirmingPassCode); // The passcode is being confirmed
        assertEquals("", activity.mPassCodeHdrExplanation.Visibility); // Explanation should be invisible
        activity.RequestPassCodeConfirmation();
        String retypedPasscode = "correctPassword";
        assertTrue(activity.verifyPasscode(newPasscode));
        assertTrue(activity.mConfirmingPassCode); // The passcode is being saved because it's correct and confirmed
    }
}
```

 For both Java and C# programs, I will generate the following unit tests. The unit tests are designed to check the correct functionality of the 'addWorker' and 'rollbackWorker' methods. I will assume that the 'terminated' state is managed separately and not tested here.

Java Unit Tests:

```java
import org.junit.Test;
import static org.junit.Assert.*;

public class WorkerManagerTest {
    private WorkerManager manager = new WorkerManager();
    private Worker w1 = new Worker(1);
    private Worker w2 = new Worker(2);

    @Test
    public void testAddWorker() {
        manager.AddWorker(w1);
        manager.AddWorker(w2);
        assertEquals(2, manager.workerCount);
        assertTrue(manager.workers.contains(w1));
        assertTrue(manager.workers.contains(w2));
    }

    @Test
    public void testRollbackWorker() {
        manager.AddWorker(w1);
        manager.AddWorker(w2);
        manager.PrintStatus();

        manager.rollbackWorker(w1);
        manager.PrintStatus();

        manager.rollbackWorker(w2);
        manager.PrintStatus();
    }
}
```

C# Unit Tests:

```csharp

using WorkerThreadRollbackExample;

[TestFixture]
public class WorkerManagerTests {
    private WorkerThreadRollbackExample example = new WorkerThreadRollbackExample();
    private Worker w1 = new Worker("Worker-1");
    private Worker w2 = new Worker("Worker-2");

    [Test]
    public void TestAddWorker() {
        example.addWorker(w1);
        example.addWorker(w2);
        Assert.AreEqual(2, example.workerCount);
        Assert.IsTrue(example.workers.Contains(w1));
        Assert.IsTrue(example.workers.Contains(w2));
    }

    [Test]
    public void TestRollbackWorker() {
        example.addWorker(w1);
        example.addWorker(w2);
        Console.WriteLine(example.ToString());

        example.rollbackWorker(w1);
        Console.WriteLine(example.ToString());

        example.rollbackWorker(w2);
        Console.WriteLine(example.ToString());
    }
}
```

```java
public class TestClass {

        statement.execute("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS dest_table (Id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, ValueSquared INTEGER)");

        string sqlInsert = $"INSERT INTO dest_table (Value) SELECT {SIMPLE_TABLE1}.Value * {SIMPLE_TABLE1}.Value FROM {SIMPLE_TABLE1};";
        statement.CommandText = sqlInsert;
        statement.ExecuteNonQuery();

        using (var r = statement.ExecuteReader()) {
```

```csharp
import java.sql.ResultSet;
import java.sql.SQLException;

public class InsertFunctionalityTest {
    public static void main(String[] args) {
        // ... (previous code remains the same)

        stmt.execute("INSERT INTO source_table VALUES (1, 10), (2, 20), (3, 30)"); // Adding more rows to check the squaring functionality

        // Create destination table and insert data from source table with expressions
        stmt.execute("CREATE TABLE dest_table (id INT, value_squared INT)");
        stmt.execute("INSERT INTO dest_table (id, value_squared) SELECT id, value * value FROM source_table");

        // Query dest_table to check if the insert worked
        ResultSet rs = stmt.executeQuery("SELECT * FROM dest_table ORDER BY id");
        while (rs.next()) {
            int id = rs.getInt("id");
            int valueSquared = rs.getInt("value_squared");
            System.out.println("id: " + id + ", value_squared: " + valueSquared);
        }
    }
}
```

```java
import org.junit.Test;

public class ProgramTest {
    @Test
    public void testPutMapEntry() throws IOException, JSONException {
        Program p = new Program();
        MapEntry entry = new MapEntry();
        entry.RetainCount = 5;
        entry.ObjectId = "object_123";

        p.PutMapEntry("local_1", entry);

        FileWrapper file = new FileWrapper(p.diskPath, "local_1");
        JSONObject json = ParseFileUtils.ReadJSONObjectFromFile(file);

        assertEquals(5, json.get(_STR));
        Assert.assertEquals("object_123", json.containsKey(_STR + "ObjectId") ? json.getString(_STR + "ObjectId") : null);
    }

    @Test(expected = InvalidOperationException.class)
    public void testPutMapEntryWithInvalidLocalId() {
        Program p = new Program();
        MapEntry entry = new MapEntry();
        entry.RetainCount = 5;
        entry.ObjectId = "object_123";

        p.PutMapEntry("not_local", entry);
    }

    @Test(expected = IOException.class)
    public void testWriteJsonToFileFailure() throws IOException {
        Program p = new Program();
        FileWrapper file = new FileWrapper(p.diskPath, "local_1");
        JSONObject json = new JSONObject();

        ParseFileUtils.WriteJSONObjectToFile(file, json);
```

 Here are the unit tests for both the Java and C# codes that perform the same task, which is to test the 'EndElement' method:

Java unit tests:

```java
public static void main(String[] args) {
    Program program = new Program();

    // Test 1: Empty end element
    program.EndElement("http://example.com", "", "");
    assert(program.m_writer.ToString().equals("</>"));

    // Test 2: Start tag open and attributes present
    program.m_attributes.Length = 3;
    program.EndElement("http://example.com", "element", "prefix:element");
    assert(program.m_writer.ToString().equals("<element xmlns=\"http://example.com\" prefix:attribute1=\"value1\" prefix:attribute2=\"value2\"/>"));

    // Test 3: No start tag open and CDATA tag open
    program.m_elemContext.m_startTagOpen = false;
    program.m_cdataTagOpen = true;
    program.EndElement("http://example.com", "", "");
    assert(program.m_writer.ToString().equals("</>")); // Close CDATA

    // Test 4: No indentation
    program.m_doIndent = false;
    program.EndElement("http://example.com", "element", "");
    assert(program.m_writer.ToString().equals("<element/>"));

    // Test 5: Indented element with multiple levels
    Program nestedProgram = new Program();
    nestedProgram.m_doIndent = true;
    program.EndElement("http://example.com", "outer", "");
    nestedProgram.EndElement("http://example.com", "inner", "");
    assert(program.m_writer.ToString().equals("<outer>\n  <inner/>\n</outer>"));
}
```

```csharp
using NUnit.Framework;
using System.Text;
using EndElementNotificationExample;

[TestFixture]
public class EndElementTests
{
    private EndElementNotificationExample handler;

    [SetUp]
    public void Setup()
    {
        handler = new EndElementNotificationExample();
    }

    [Test]
    public void Test1_EmptyEndElement()
    {
        SAXParserFactory factory = SAXParserFactory.NewInstance();
        SAXParser saxParser = factory.NewSAXParser();
        StringReader xml = new StringReader("</>");
        handler.Reset();
        saxParser.Parse(xml, handler);
        Assert.AreEqual("<>", handler.ToString());
    }

    [Test]
    public void Test2_StartTagOpenAndAttributesPresent()
    {
        SAXParserFactory factory = SAXParserFactory.NewInstance();
        SAXParser saxParser = factory.NewSAXParser();
        StringReader xml = new StringReader("<root attr1=\"value1\" attr2=\"value2\"><child>content</child></root>");
        handler.Reset();
        saxParser.Parse(xml, handler);
        Assert.AreEqual("<root attr1=\"value1\" attr2=\"value2\">content</root>", handler.ToString());
    }

    [Test]
    public void Test3_NoStartTagOpenAndCDATA TagOpen()
    {
        SAXParserFactory factory = SAXParserFactory.NewInstance();
        SAXParser saxParser = factory.NewSAXParser();
        StringReader xml = new StringReader("<![CDATA[Content]]>");
        handler.Reset();
        saxParser.Parse(xml, handler);
        Assert.AreEqual("</>", handler.ToString()); // Close CDATA
    }

    [Test]
    public void Test4_NoIndentation()
    {
        SAXParserFactory factory = SAXParserFactory.NewInstance();
        SAXParser saxParser = factory.NewSAXParser();
        StringReader xml = new StringReader("<root><child/></root>");
        handler.m_doIndent = false;
        handler.Reset();
        saxParser.Parse(xml, handler);
        Assert.AreEqual("<root><child/></root>", handler.ToString());
    }

    [Test]
    public void Test5_IndentedElementWithMultipleLevels()
    {
        EndElementNotificationExample nestedHandler = new EndElementNotificationExample();
        SAXParserFactory factory = SAXParserFactory.NewInstance();
        SAXParser saxParser = factory.NewSAXParser();
        StringReader xml = new StringReader("<outer><inner/></outer>");
        handler.m_doIndent = true;
        nestedHandler.Reset();
        handler.EndElement("", "outer", "");
        handler.EndElement("", "inner", "");
        Assert.AreEqual("<outer>\n  <inner/>\n</outer>", handler.ToString());
    }
}
```

```java
import org.junit.Test;

public class LoggerTests {
    @Test
    public void testFatalErrorWithParams() {
        String expectedOutput = "FATAL [Main]: FATAL ERROR: An unrecoverable error occurred.";
        Logger.F("FATAL ERROR: An unrecoverable error occurred.");
        assertEquals(expectedOutput, Logger.getLog());
    }

    @Test
    public void testFatalErrorNoParams() {
        String expectedOutput = "FATAL [Main]: Another fatal error with no params";
        Logger.F("Another fatal error with no params");
        assertEquals(expectedOutput, Logger.getLog());
    }

    @Test
    public void testFatalErrorWrongLevel() {
        Logger.allowWtf = false;
        String expectedOutput = "";
        Logger.F("This is a fatal error: Disk not found");
        assertEquals(expectedOutput, Logger.getLog());
    }

    @Test
    public void testGetCallerStackTraceElement() {
        String actualOutput = Logger.GetCallerStackTraceElement();
        String expectedOutput = "Main";
        assertEquals(expectedOutput, actualOutput);
    }

    @Test
    public void testGenerateTag() {
        String actualOutput = Logger.GenerateTag("Main");
        String expectedOutput = "Tag_Main";
        assertEquals(expectedOutput, actualOutput);
    }
}
```

Original Programs:

Java:
```java
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;

public class CatalogTreeLeaf
{
    private string _name;

    public CatalogTreeLeaf(string name)
    {
        _name = name;
    }

    public string GetName()
    {
        return _name;
    }
}

public class CatalogTree
{
    private List<CatalogTreeLeaf> _leaves;
    private int _NUM = 0;

    public CatalogTree()
    {
        _leaves = new List<CatalogTreeLeaf>();
    }

    public void AddLeaf(CatalogTreeLeaf leaf)
    {
        _leaves.Add(leaf);
    }

    // leaves can be used for many - to - many relations
    public void DeleteLeaves(string name)
    {
        for (int i = _NUM; i < _leaves.Count; i++)
        {
            CatalogTreeLeaf leaf = _leaves[i];
            if (name.Equals(leaf.GetName()))
            {
                _leaves.RemoveAt(i);
                i--; // Adjust index after removal
            }
        }
    }

    public void PrintLeaves()
    {
        foreach (var leaf in _leaves)
        {
            Console.WriteLine(leaf.GetName());
        }
    }
}

public class Program
{
    public static void Main()
    {
        CatalogTree tree = new CatalogTree();
        tree.AddLeaf(new CatalogTreeLeaf("Apple"));
        tree.AddLeaf(new CatalogTreeLeaf("Banana"));
        tree.AddLeaf(new CatalogTreeLeaf("Apple"));
        tree.AddLeaf(new CatalogTreeLeaf("Cherry"));
        tree.AddLeaf(new CatalogTreeLeaf("Apple"));

        Console.WriteLine("Before deleting 'Apple':");
        tree.PrintLeaves();

        tree.DeleteLeaves("Apple");

        Console.WriteLine("\nAfter deleting 'Apple':");
        tree.PrintLeaves();
    }
}
```
C#:
```csharp
import java.util.*;

public class Main {
    // removes the given element from the list of leaves
    public static void removeLeaf(List<String> leaves, String leaf) {
        leaves.remove(leaf);
    }

    public static void main(String[] args) {
        List<String> leaves = new ArrayList<>(Arrays.asList("oak", "maple", "birch", "elm"));
        System.out.println("Before removal: " + leaves);
        removeLeaf(leaves, "maple");
        System.out.println("After removal: " + leaves);
    }
}
```

 Here are the unit tests for both the Java and C# codes. The unit tests check if the provided characters are valid for use in a URI component based on the generous validation described in the code comments.

Java:

```java
public class UriValidatorTest {
    private static final char[] CHARS = new char[]{'a', 'A', '0', '-', '.', '_', '~', '!', '$', '&', '\'', '(', ')', '*', '+', ',', ';', '=', ':', '@', '%', '/', '['];

    public void testValidComponentChars() {
        UriValidator validator = new UriValidator();
        BitArray generous = new BitArray(128, false);
        for (char c : CHARS) {
            if (validator.Validate(new char[]{c}, 0, 0, generous)) {
                System.out.println("Char '" + c + "' is valid");
            } else {
                System.out.println("Char '" + c + "' is not valid");
            }
        }
    }
}
```

C#:

```csharp
using NUnit.Framework;
using System;
using System.Collections;

[TestFixture]
public class UriCharacterValidatorTest {
    private static readonly char[] CHARS = new char[]{'a', 'A', '0', '-', '.', '_', '~', '!', '$', '&', '\'', '(', ')', '*', '+', ',', ';', '=', ':', '@', '%', '/', '['];

    [Test]
    public void TestValidComponentChars() {
        Assert.AreEqual(true, UriCharacterValidator.isValidComponentChar('a'));
        Assert.AreEqual(true, UriCharacterValidator.isValidComponentChar('A'));
        Assert.AreEqual(true, UriCharacterValidator.isValidComponentChar('0'));
        Assert.AreEqual(true, UriCharacterValidator.isValidComponentChar('-'));
        Assert.AreEqual(true, UriCharacterValidator.isValidComponentChar('.'));
        Assert.AreEqual(true, UriCharacterValidator.isValidComponentChar('_'));
        Assert.AreEqual(true, UriCharacterValidator.isValidComponentChar('~'));
        Assert.AreEqual(true, UriCharacterValidator.isValidComponentChar('!'));
        Assert.AreEqual(true, UriCharacterValidator.isValidComponentChar('$'));
        Assert.AreEqual(true, UriCharacterValidator.isValidComponentChar('&'));
        Assert.AreEqual(true, UriCharacterValidator.isValidComponentChar('\''));
        Assert.AreEqual(true, UriCharacterValidator.isValidComponentChar('('));
        Assert.AreEqual(true, UriCharacterValidator.isValidComponentChar(')'));
        Assert.AreEqual(true, UriCharacterValidator.isValidComponentChar('*'));
        Assert.AreEqual(true, UriCharacterValidator.isValidComponentChar('+'));
        Assert.AreEqual(true, UriCharacterValidator.isValidComponentChar ',');
        Assert.AreEqual(true, UriCharacterValidator.isValidComponentChar(';'));
        Assert.AreEqual(true, UriCharacterValidator.isValidComponentChar('='));
        Assert.AreEqual(true, UriCharacterValidator.isValidComponentChar(':'));
        Assert.AreEqual(true, UriCharacterValidator.isValidComponentChar('@'));
        Assert.AreEqual(true, UriCharacterValidator.isValidComponentChar('%'));
```

 Here are the unit tests for both the Java and C# programs. I've created a test class called `ShutdownHooksTest` that contains five methods to test the removal of a previously registered shutdown hook. The unit tests assume the existence of a method called `assertFalse(boolean expected)` which asserts that the provided boolean value is false, and `assertEquals(Object expected, Object actual)` which asserts that the provided expected and actual objects are equal.

Java:
```java
import org.junit.Test;
import static org.junit.Assert.*;

public class ShutdownHooksTest {
    @Test
    public void testRemoveUnregisteredShutdownHook() {
        ShutdownHooksManager manager = new ShutdownHooksManager();
        Thread hook = new Thread(() -> System.out.println("Shutdown hook running..."));
        boolean removed = manager.RemoveShutdownHook(hook);
        assertFalse(removed);
    }

    @Test
    public void testRemoveNullShutdownHook() {
        ShutdownHooksManager manager = new ShutdownHooksManager();
        Thread hook = null;
        boolean removed = manager.RemoveShutdownHook(hook);
        assertFalse(removed);
    }

    @Test
    public void testRemoveRegisteredShutdownHook() {
        ShutdownHooksManager manager = new ShutdownHooksManager();
        Thread hook = new Thread(() -> System.out.println("Shutdown hook running..."));
        manager.RegisterShutdownHook(hook);
        boolean removed = manager.RemoveShutdownHook(hook);
        assertTrue(removed);
    }

    @Test
    public void testRemoveAlreadyRemovedShutdownHook() {
        ShutdownHooksManager manager = new ShutdownHooksManager();
        Thread hook = new Thread(() -> System.out.println("Shutdown hook running..."));
        manager.RegisterShutdownHook(hook);
        boolean removed1 = manager.RemoveShutdownHook(hook);
        assertTrue(removed1);
        boolean removed2 = manager.RemoveShutdownHook(hook);
        assertFalse(removed2);
    }

    @Test
    public void testRegisterAndRemoveShutdownHook() {
        ShutdownHooksManager manager = new ShutdownHooksManager();
        Thread hook = new Thread(() -> System.out.println("Shutdown hook running..."));
        boolean registered = manager.RegisterShutdownHook(hook);
        assertTrue(registered);
        boolean removed = manager.RemoveShutdownHook(hook);
        assertTrue(removed);
    }
}
```

C#:

```csharp
using NUnit.Framework;
using System;

public class ShutdownHooksTest {
    [Test]
    public void Test_RemoveUnregisteredShutdownHook() {
        ShutdownHooksManager manager = new ShutdownHooksManager();
        Thread hook = new Thread(new Runnable(() => Console.WriteLine("Shutdown hook running...")));
        Assert.IsFalse(manager.RemoveShutdownHook(hook));
    }

    [Test]
    public void Test_RemoveNullShutdownHook() {
        ShutdownHooksManager manager = new ShutdownHooksManager();
        Thread hook = null;
        Assert.IsFalse(manager.RemoveShutdownHook(hook));
    }

    [Test]
    public void Test_RemoveRegisteredShutdownHook() {
        ShutdownHooksManager manager = new ShutdownHooksManager();
        Thread hook = new Thread(new Runnable(() => Console.WriteLine("Shutdown hook running...")));
        manager.RegisterShutdownHook(hook);
        Assert.IsTrue(manager.RemoveShutdownHook(hook));
    }

    [Test]
    public void Test_RemoveAlreadyRemovedShutdownHook() {
        ShutdownHooksManager manager = new ShutdownHooksManager();
        Thread hook = new Thread(new Runnable(() => Console.WriteLine("Shutdown hook running...")));
        manager.RegisterShutdownHook(hook);
        bool removed1 = manager.RemoveShutdownHook(hook);
        Assert.IsTrue(removed1);
        bool removed2 = manager.RemoveShutdownHook(hook);
        Assert.IsFalse(removed2);
    }

    [Test]
    public void Test_RegisterAndRemoveShutdownHook() {
        ShutdownHooksManager manager = new ShutdownHooksManager();
        Thread hook = new Thread(new Runnable(() => Console.WriteLine("Shutdown hook running...")));
        bool registered = manager.RegisterShutdownHook(hook);
        Assert.IsTrue(registered);
        bool removed = manager.RemoveShutdownHook(hook);
        Assert.IsTrue(removed);
    }
}
```

```java
import org.junit.Test;
import static org.junit.Assert.*;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

public class KeyboardInputManagerTest {
    private KeyboardInputManager kim;

    @Test
    public void testRequestInput() throws InterruptedException {
        kim = new KeyboardInputManager();
        kim.Start();
        Thread.sleep(100); // Give the thread time to start
        String input = "Hello World";
        Console.WriteLine("Type something, type 'quit' to exit:");
        Console.Write("Input> ");
```

```java
import org.junit.Assert;
import org.junit.Test;

public class StackScrollerTest {

    private final float EPSILON = 0.0f;

    @Test
    public void testInitialScroll() {
        StackScroller scroller = new StackScroller(120.0f);
        Assert.assertEquals(120.0f, scroller.GetStackScroll(), EPSILON);
    }

    @Test
    public void testBoundScrollFalse() {
        StackScroller scroller = new StackScroller(50.0f);
        boolean wasBound = scroller.BoundScroll();
        Assert.assertFalse(wasBound);
    }

    @Test
    public void testBoundScrollTrue() {
        StackScroller scroller = new StackScroller(121.0f);
        boolean wasBound = scroller.BoundScroll();
        Assert.assertTrue(wasBound);
        Assert.assertEquals(100.0f, scroller.GetStackScroll(), EPSILON);
    }

    @Test
    public void testBoundScrollMin() {
        StackScroller scroller = new StackScroller(0.0f - EPSILON);
        boolean wasBound = scroller.BoundScroll();
        Assert.assertTrue(wasBound);
        Assert.assertEquals(0.0f, scroller.GetStackScroll(), EPSILON);
    }

    @Test
    public void testBoundScrollMax() {
        StackScroller scroller = new StackScroller(100.0f + EPSILON);
        boolean wasBound = scroller.BoundScroll();
        Assert.assertTrue(wasBound);
        Assert.assertEquals(100.0f, scroller.GetStackScroll(), EPSILON);
    }
}
```

 Here are the unit tests for both the Java and C# programs. I have generated them using JUnit for Java and NUnit for C#. The unit tests perform the same task, which is to test the `Aggregate` method in the `MyEvaluation` class by creating two evaluations, `eval1` and `eval2`, adding `eval2` to `eval1`, and then checking if the resulting evaluation (`eval1`) matches the expected outcome.

Java:
```java
import org.junit.Test;
import static org.junit.Assert.*;

public class TestMyEvaluation {
    @Test
    public void testAggregate() {
        Evaluation eval1 = new MyEvaluation(2);
        eval1.m_Incorrect = 1;
        eval1.m_Correct = 2;
        eval1.m_Unclassified = 3;
        eval1.m_MissingClass = 5;
        eval1.m_WithClass = 6;
        double[][] confusionMatrix = new double[][]{
            {1, 2},
            {3, 4}
        };
        double[] classPriors = new double[]{0.3, 0.7};
        eval1.m_ConfusionMatrix = confusionMatrix;
        eval1.m_ClassPriors = classPriors;
        eval1.m_ClassPriorsSum = 1.0;

        MyEvaluation eval2 = new MyEvaluation(2);
        eval2.m_Incorrect = 5;
        eval2.m_Correct = 3;
        eval2.m_Unclassified = 0;
        eval2.m_MissingClass = 8;
        eval2.m_WithClass = 7;
        double[][] newConfusionMatrix = new double[][]{
            {6, 7},
            {9, 10}
        };
        double[] newClassPriors = new double[]{0.4, 0.6};
        eval2.m_ConfusionMatrix = newConfusionMatrix;
        eval2.m_ClassPriors = newClassPriors;
        eval2.m_ClassPriorsSum = 1.0;

        eval1.Aggregate(eval2);

        assertEquals("Incorrect should be: 6", 6, eval1.m_Incorrect);
        assertEquals("Correct should be: 5", 5, eval1.m_Correct);
        assertEquals("Unclassified should be: 3", 3, eval1.m_Unclassified);
        assertEquals("MissingClass should be: 13", 13, eval1.m_MissingClass);
        assertEquals("WithClass should be: 13", 13, eval1.m_WithClass);
        double[][] expectedConfusionMatrix = new double[][]{
            {7, 9},
            {10, 12},
            {1, 2},
            {3, 4}
        };
        for (int i = 0; i < expectedConfusionMatrix.length; i++) {
            for (int j = 0; j < expectedConfusionMatrix[i].length; j++) {
                assertEquals("Expected confusion matrix [" + i + "][" + j + "]: " + expectedConfusionMatrix[i][j], expectedConfusionMatrix[i][j], eval1.m_ConfusionMatrix[i][j]);
            }
        }
        double[] expectedClassPriors = new double[]{0.3, 0.7};
        for (int i = 0; i < expectedClassPriors.length; i++) {
            assertEquals("Expected class priors [" + i + "]: " + expectedClassPriors[i], expectedClassPriors[i], eval1.m_ClassPriors[i]);
        }
        assertEquals("Expected class prior sum: 1.0", 1.0, eval1.m_ClassPriorsSum);
    }
}
```

```csharp
public class TestMyEvaluation

        eval1.m_Correct = 2;
        eval1.m_Unclassified = 3;
        eval1.m_MissingClass = 5;
        eval1.m_WithClass = 6;
        double[][] confusionMatrix = new double[2][];
        confusionMatrix[0] = new double[] { 1, 2 };
        confusionMatrix[1] = new double[] { 3, 4 };
        var classPriors = new double[] { 0.3, 0.7 };
        eval1.m_ConfusionMatrix = confusionMatrix;
        eval1.m_ClassPriors = classPriors;
        eval1.m_ClassPriorsSum = 1.0;
```

```java
import org.junit.Test;
import static org.junit.Assert.*;

public class VolumeScannerTest {
    @Test
    public void testScan_WithVisibleCopiesAndRsets_ShouldPrintCorrectly() {
        CGRequestParams request = new CGRequestParams();
        List<String> copies = Arrays.asList("Copy1", "Copy2");
        List<String> rsets = Arrays.asList("Rset1", "Rset2");
        request.setCopies(copies);
        request.setRsets(rsets);

        VolumeScanner scanner = new VolumeScanner();
        scanner.WaitForVolumesToBeVisible(request);
    }

    @Test
    public void testScan_WithInvisibleCopiesAndRsets_ShouldPrintCorrectly() {
        CGRequestParams request = new CGRequestParams();
        List<String> copies = Arrays.asList("Copy1", "Copy2");
        List<String> rsets = Arrays.asList("Rset1", "Rset2");
        request.setCopies(copies);
        request.setRsets(rsets);

        VolumeScanner scanner = new VolumeScanner();
        Site site = new Site("Site1");
        for (String copy : copies) {
            site.addVisibleVolume(new Volume(copy));
        }
        for (String rset : rsets) {
            site.addVisibleVolume(new Volume(rset));
        }
        List<Site> sites = Arrays.asList(site);
        scanner.WaitForVolumesToBeVisible(request, sites);
    }
```

C#:

```csharp
using Xunit;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System;

public class RecoverPointScannerTests
{
    [Fact]
    public void TestScan_WithVisibleCopiesAndRsets_ShouldPrintCorrectly()
    {
        var request = new RecoverPointScannerTests.RequestParams();
        var copies = new List<string> { "Copy1", "Copy2" };
        var rsets = new List<string> { "Rset1", "Rset2" };
        request.Copies = copies;
        request.Rsets = rsets;

        var scanner = new RecoverPointScanner();
        scanner.ScanAllSitesUntilAllVolumesVisible(CreateSite("Site1"), copies, rsets);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void TestScan_WithInvisibleCopiesAndRsets_ShouldPrintCorrectly()
    {
        var request = new RecoverPointScannerTests.RequestParams();
        var copies = new List<string> { "Copy1", "Copy2" };
        var rsets = new List<string> { "Rset1", "Rset2" };
        request.Copies = copies;
        request.Rsets = rsets;

        var site = CreateSite("Site1");
        foreach (var copy in copies)
            site.AddVisibleVolume(new Volume(copy));
        foreach (var rset in rsets)
            site.AddVisibleVolume(new Volume(rset));

        var scanner = new RecoverPointScanner();
        scanner.ScanAllSitesUntilAllVolumesVisible(site, copies, rsets);
    }

    private Site CreateSite(string name)
    {
        return new Site { Name = name };
    }

    public class RequestParams : IRequestParams
    {
        public List<Volume> Copies { get; set; }
        public List<Volume> Rsets { get; set; }
    }

    public interface IRequestParams
    {
        List<Volume> Copies { get; set; }
        List<Volume> Rsets { get; set; }
    }

    public class Volume
    {
        public string Name { get; private set; }

        public Volume(string name)
        {
            this.Name = name;
        }

        public override bool Equals(object obj)
        {
            if (this == obj) return true;
            if (!(obj is Volume)) return false;
            var volume = (Volume)obj;
            return Name != null ? Name.Equals(volume.Name) : volume.Name == null;
        }

        public override int GetHashCode()
        {
            return Name != null ? Name.GetHashCode() : 0;
        }
    }

    public class Site : IRequestParams
    {
        public string Name { get; private set; }
        public List<Volume> VisibleVolumes { get; private set; } = new List<Volume>();

        public Site(string name)
        {
            this.Name = name;
        }

        public void AddVisibleVolume(Volume volume)
        {
            this.VisibleVolumes.Add(volume);
        }

        public bool IsVolumeVisible(Volume volume)
        {
            return VisibleVolumes.Contains(volume);
        }
    }
}
```

 For both Java and C# programs, I will create unit tests that verify the `UpdateView` method updates the outline view correctly. The unit tests consist of adding a new node and updating an existing one. Here are the unit tests for both the Java and C# programs:

```java
import org.junit.Test;
import static org.junit.Assert.*;
import java.util.Arrays;

public class OutlineViewTests {
    private OutlineView view;

    @Test
    public void testAddNewNode() {
        view = new OutlineView("inputFile1", new Object());
        view.UpdateView();
        assertEquals(2, view.fStateMap.size()); // initial state with 2 nodes
        view.UpdateView("newNode", "Test Node");
        assertEquals(3, view.fStateMap.size()); // added new node after UpdateView call
    }

    @Test
    public void testUpdateExistingNode() {
        view = new OutlineView("inputFile1", new Object());
        view.UpdateView();
        String originalValue = (String) view.fStateMap.get("inputFile1").JsonObject.Data["field1"]; // get the initial value of field1
        view.UpdateView("inputFile1", "Updated Value");
        assertNotEquals(originalValue, (String) view.fStateMap.get("inputFile1").JsonObject.Data["field1"]); // verify that the value was updated
    }
}
```

```csharp
        Assert.AreNotEqual(originalValue, updatedValue);
```

CODE COUNT: 39

 Here are the unit tests for both the Java and C# programs. The unit tests will check if the image is correctly painted at the specified coordinates.

Java:

```java
import org.junit.Test;
import static org.junit.Assert.assertArrayEquals;
import java.awt.image.BufferedImage;
import java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream;
import java.io.IOException;

public class ImagePaintingFormTest {
    @Test
    public void testPaint() throws IOException {
        ImagePaintingForm form = new ImagePaintingForm();
        BufferedImage expectedImage = new BufferedImage(200, 200, BufferedImage.TYPE_INT_ARGB);
        Graphics2D g = expectedImage.createGraphics();
        g.setColor(Color.LIGHT_GRAY);
        g.fillRect(0, 0, 200, 200);
        g.setColor(Color.BLACK);
        g.drawOval(10, 10, 80, 80);
        int[] expectedPixels = getPixelsFromImage(expectedImage);
        form.OnPaint(createGraphicsForTest());
        Thread.yield(); // give time for the painting to complete
        BufferedImage actualImage = (BufferedImage) form.image;
        int[] actualPixels = getPixelsFromImage(actualImage);
        assertArrayEquals("Images do not match", expectedPixels, actualPixels);
    }

    private Graphics2D createGraphicsForTest() {
        ByteArrayOutputStream os = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
        BufferedImage image = new BufferedImage(1, 1, BufferedImage.TYPE_3BYTE_BGR);
        Graphics2D g = image.createGraphics();
        g.setComposite(AlphaComposite.Src);
        return g;
    }

    private int[] getPixelsFromImage(BufferedImage image) {
        int width = image.getWidth();
        int height = image.getHeight();
        int[] pixels = new int[width * height];
        image.getRGB(0, 0, width, height, pixels, 0, width);
        return pixels;
    }
}
```

C#:

```csharp
using System;
using System.Drawing;
using Xunit;
using static System.BufferedImageExtension;
using static System.Drawing.BitmapExtensions;

public class ImagePainterTest
{
    [Fact]
    public void TestPaint()
    {
        using (var image = new Bitmap(200, 200))
        {
            using (var g = Graphics.FromImage(image))
            {
                g.Clear(Color.LightGray);
                g.FillRectangle(new Rectangle(0, 0, 200, 200), Brushes.LightGray);
                g.DrawEllipse(Pens.Black, 10, 10, 80, 80);
            }

            var expectedPixels = image.GetPixelArray();
            ImagePainter panel = new ImagePainter(image);
            Assert.Equal("Images do not match", expectedPixels, panel.GetPixelArray());
        }
    }
}
```

Both the Java and C# programs contain a single unit test that checks if the image is painted correctly at the specified coordinates. The tests first create an expected image with the correct colors and shapes, then they compare the pixels of the actual image obtained from the program to the expected image's pixels.

 Here are the unit tests for both the Java and C# codes, preserving the original program structure. The unit tests are designed to test the `ConnectionAllowed` method with different event names and also to check if adding and removing listeners work correctly.

Java:
```java
import org.junit.Test;
import static org.junit.Assert.*;

public class EventManagerTest {
    @Test
    public void testConnectionAllowedEmpty() {
        MyEventManager em = new MyEventManager();
        assertTrue(em.ConnectionAllowed("TestEvent"));
    }

    @Test
    public void testConnectionAllowedAddedListener() {
        MyEventManager em = new MyEventManager();
        em.AddListener("TestEvent", () -> System.out.println("TestEvent fired!"));
        assertTrue(em.ConnectionAllowed("TestEvent"));
    }

    @Test
    public void testConnectionAllowedRemovedListener() {
        MyEventManager em = new MyEventManager();
        em.AddListener("TestEvent", () -> System.out.println("TestEvent fired!"));
        em.RemoveListener("TestEvent", () -> System.out.println("TestEvent fired!"));
        assertTrue(em.ConnectionAllowed("TestEvent"));
    }

    @Test
    public void testConnectionAllowedNonExistentListener() {
        MyEventManager em = new MyEventManager();
        assertFalse(em.ConnectionAllowed("NonExistentEvent"));
    }
}
```

```java
import org.junit.Assert;
import org.junit.Test;

public class SeriesRendererTest {
    @Test
    public void testMultipleRendererAddSeriesRenderer_Java() {
        MultipleRenderer multipleRenderer = new MultipleRenderer();
        SimpleSeriesRenderer renderer1 = new SimpleSeriesRenderer("Renderer A");
        SimpleSeriesRenderer renderer2 = new SimpleSeriesRenderer("Renderer B");
        SimpleSeriesRenderer renderer3 = new SimpleSeriesRenderer("Renderer C");

        multipleRenderer.AddSeriesRenderer(0, renderer1);
        multipleRenderer.AddSeriesRenderer(1, renderer2);
        multipleRenderer.AddSeriesRenderer(1, renderer3); // renderer3 will be placed at index 1

        List<SimpleSeriesRenderer> expectedRenderers = new ArrayList<>();

        expectedRenderers.add(renderer2);
        expectedRenderers.add(renderer3);

        Assert.assertEquals(expectedRenderers, multipleRenderer.mRenderers);

    @Test
    public void testMultipleRendererPrintRenderers_Java() {
        MultipleRenderer multipleRenderer = new MultipleRenderer();
        SimpleSeriesRenderer renderer1 = new SimpleSeriesRenderer("Renderer A");
        SimpleSeriesRenderer renderer2 = new SimpleSeriesRenderer("Renderer B");
        SimpleSeriesRenderer renderer3 = new SimpleSeriesRenderer("Renderer C");

        multipleRenderer.AddSeriesRenderer(0, renderer1);
        multipleRenderer.AddSeriesRenderer(1, renderer2);
        multipleRenderer.AddSeriesRenderer(1, renderer3); // renderer3 will be placed at index 1

        String expectedOutput = "Index 0: Renderer A\n" +
                "Index 1: Renderer B\n" +
                "Index 2: Renderer C\n";

        multipleRenderer.PrintRenderers();
        String actualOutput = multipleRenderer.ToString().trim();
```

```csharp
public class SeriesRendererTest {
```

 Here are the unit tests for both the Java and C# programs. I've generated them to test the copy functionality with different buffer sizes, total file size, and interruption scenarios.

Java:

```java
import org.junit.Test;
import static org.junit.Assert.*;

public class StreamCopierTest {
    private byte[] sourceData = new byte[1024];
    for (int i = 0; i < sourceData.length; i++)
        sourceData[i] = (byte)(i % 256);

    @Test
    public void testCopy() throws Exception {
        CopyListener listener = new ProgressCopyListener();

        // Test with different buffer sizes
        StreamCopier.CopyStream(new MemoryStream(sourceData), new MemoryStream(), listener, 128);
        StreamCopier.CopyStream(new MemoryStream(sourceData), new MemoryStream(), listener, 64);
        StreamCopier.CopyStream(new MemoryStream(sourceData), new MemoryStream(), listener, sourceData.length);

        // Test total file size larger than default
        byte[] largeSourceData = new byte[10 * 1024 * 1024]; // 10MB
        CopyListener largeListener = new ProgressCopyListener();
        StreamCopier.CopyStream(new MemoryStream(largeSourceData), new MemoryStream(), largeListener, sourceData.length);

        // Test interruption scenario
        MemoryStream outputInterrupt = new MemoryStream();
        int bufferSizeInterrupt = 128;
        CopyListener interruptListener = new ProgressCopyListener();
        StreamCopier.CopyStream(new MemoryStream(sourceData), outputInterrupt, interruptListener, bufferSizeInterrupt);
        assertEquals("Copied 0/1024 bytes.", outputInterrupt.ToString()); // Interrupted before any data is written
    }
}
```

C#:

```csharp
using System;
using Xunit;
using java.io;

public class MainTest {
    private byte[] sampleData = new byte[10_000];
    static Random random = new Random();
    public MainTest() {
        new random.NextBytes(sampleData);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void TestCopy() {
        ProgressListener listener = new ExampleProgressListener(5_000); // Interrupt after 5000 bytes

        using (var inStream = new MemoryStream(sampleData))
        using (var outStream = new MemoryStream()) {
            long bytesCopied = InterruptibleStreamCopier.copy(inStream, outStream, listener);
            Assert.Equal(5_000, bytesCopied); // Interrupted before the entire file is copied
        }

        using (var inStream = new MemoryStream(sampleData))
        using (var outStream = new MemoryStream()) {
            ProgressListener noInterruptListener = new ExampleProgressListener(sampleData.Length + 1); // Don't interrupt
            long bytesCopied = InterruptibleStreamCopier.copy(inStream, outStream, noInterruptListener);
            Assert.Equal(sampleData.Length, bytesCopied); // Entire file is copied without interruption
        }
    }
}
```

The unit tests are written using JUnit for Java and xUnit (specifically Xunit) for C#. Both test suites include tests for different buffer sizes, total file size larger than the default value, and an interruption scenario. You can run these tests on your own Java and C# projects to verify the functionality of each implementation.

```java
import org.junit.Test;
import static org.junit.Assert.assertEquals;

public class AngleCalculatorTest {
    @Test
    public void testGetYValueSum() {
        float[] values = {10, 20, 30, 40};
        Data data = new Data(values);
        float sum = data.GetYValueSum();
        assertEquals(100f, sum, 0.001); // Tolerance for floating-point comparison
    }

    @Test
    public void testCalcAngleWithValidInput() {
        float[] values = {10, 20, 30, 40};
        Data data = new Data(values);
        AngleCalculator calculator = new AngleCalculator(data);
        float value = 20;
        float angle = calculator.CalcAngle(value);
        assertEquals(90f, angle, 0.001); // Tolerance for floating-point comparison
    }

    @Test(expected = NullPointerException.class)
    public void testCalcAngleWithNullData() {
        AngleCalculator calculator = new AngleCalculator(null);
        calculator.RunExample(20); // This should throw a NullPointerException if mData is null
    }

    @Test
    public void testRunExampleWithValidInput() {
        float[] values = {10, 20, 30, 40};
        Data data = new Data(values);
        AngleCalculator calculator = new AngleCalculator(data);
        float value = 20;
        calculator.RunExample(value); // Verify that the correct angle is printed
    }

    @Test
    public void testRunExampleWithEdgeCase() {
        float[] values = {1, 1};
        Data data = new Data(values);
        AngleCalculator calculator = new AngleCalculator(data);
        float value = 1;
        calculator.RunExample(value); // Verify that the correct angle is printed for edge case (sum of y values = 2)
    }
}
```

CODE COUNT: 47

 Here are the unit tests for both Java and C# programs. I've created an interface named `IBufferPool` to facilitate testing the common behavior of both versions. The test cases are the same for both languages.

Java:
```java
import org.junit.Test;
import static org.junit.Assert.*;
import java.util.Arrays;

public class BufferPoolTest {
    private IBufferPool pool;

    @Test
    public void testReturnBuf() {
        pool = new BufferPool(100);

        byte[] buffer1 = new byte[20];
        byte[] buffer2 = new byte[30];
        byte[] buffer3 = new byte[15];
        byte[] buffer4 = new byte[50];
        byte[] buffer5 = new byte[200];
        byte[] nullBuffer = null;

        pool.ReturnBuf(buffer1);
        pool.ReturnBuf(buffer2);
        pool.ReturnBuf(buffer3);
        pool.ReturnBuf(buffer4); // This should trim the oldest buffers if over the size limit
        pool.ReturnBuf(nullBuffer);         // Should be ignored
        pool.ReturnBuf(buffer5); // Too big, should be ignored

        pool.PrintStatus();

        assertEquals(102, pool.getCurrentSize());
        assertEquals(4, pool.getBuffersByLastUse().size());
        int[] expectedSortedSizes = {15, 30, 20, 50};
        List<Integer> actualSortedSizes = new ArrayList<>();
        for (byte[] buf : pool.getBuffersBySize()) {
            actualSortedSizes.add(buf.length);
        }
        Arrays.sort(expectedSortedSizes);
        Arrays.sort(actualSortedSizes.stream().mapToInt(i -> i).toArray());
        assertArrayEquals(expectedSortedSizes, actualSortedSizes.stream().mapToInt(i -> i).toArray());
    }
}
```

C#:
```csharp
using System;
using Microsoft.VisualStudio.TestTools.UnitTesting;
using static BufferPool;

[TestClass]
public class BufferPoolTests {
    private IBufferPool pool;

    [TestMethod]
    public void TestReturnBuf() {
        pool = new BufferPool(100);

        byte[] buffer1 = new byte[20];
        byte[] buffer2 = new byte[30];
        byte[] buffer3 = new byte[15];
        byte[] buffer4 = new byte[50];
        byte[] nullBuffer = null;
        byte[] buffer5 = new byte[200];

        pool.ReturnBuf(buffer1);
        pool.ReturnBuf(buffer2);
        pool.ReturnBuf(buffer3);
        pool.ReturnBuf(buffer4); // This should trim the oldest buffers if over the size limit
        pool.ReturnBuf(nullBuffer);         // Should be ignored
        pool.ReturnBuf(buffer5); // Too big, should be ignored

        pool.PrintStatus();

        Assert.AreEqual(102, pool.CurrentSize);
        Assert.AreEqual(4, pool.BuffersByLastUse.Count);
        int[] expectedSortedSizes = {15, 30, 20, 50};
        int[] actualSortedSizes = Enumerable.ToArray(pool.BuffersBySize.OrderBy(b => b.Length));
        CollectionAssert.AreEqual(expectedSortedSizes, actualSortedSizes);
    }
}
```
