CODE COUNT: 48

 Here are the unit tests for both the Java and C# code. I've added a `UnitTest` class in Java and a `Program` class in C# to contain the unit tests. The unit tests are designed to test the creation of a new instance, adding options to an existing base instance, and verifying the values in the resulting instance.

Java:

```java
import org.junit.Test;
import static org.junit.Assert.assertEquals;

public class UnitTest {
    @Test
    public void testCreateNewInstance() {
        MyOptions baseOptions = new MyOptions();
        baseOptions.ValueMap.put("existing", "value");

        List<MyOptionValue> values = new ArrayList<>();
        values.add(new MyOptionValue("a", 1));
        values.add(new MyOptionValue("b", 2));

        Func<MyOptionValue, MyOptionValue> filter = v -> new MyOptionValue(v.Key, v.Value);

        MyOptions myOptions = new MyOptions();
        myOptions.CreateNewInstance(filter, baseOptions, values);

        assertEquals("existing: value", baseOptions.ValueMap.get("existing"));
        assertEquals("a: 1", myOptions.ValueMap.get("a"));
        assertEquals("b: 2", myOptions.ValueMap.get("b"));
    }
}
```

C#:

```csharp
using Xunit;

namespace UnitTest
{
    public class Test
    {
        [Fact]
        public void TestCreateNewInstance()
        {
            var baseOptions = new MyOptions();
            baseOptions.ValueMap["existing"] = "value";

            IEnumerable<MyOptionValue> values = new List<MyOptionValue>
            {
                new MyOptionValue("a", 1),
                new MyOptionValue("b", 2)
            };

            Func<MyOptionValue, MyOptionValue> filter = v => new MyOptionValue(v.Key, v.Value);

            var myOptions = new MyOptions();
            myOptions.CreateNewInstance(filter, baseOptions, values);

            Assert.Equal("value", baseOptions.ValueMap["existing"]);
            Assert.Equal(1, myOptions.ValueMap["a"].Value);
            Assert.Equal(2, myOptions.ValueMap["b"].Value);
        }
    }
}
```

 For both Java and C# programs, I'll generate the following unit tests to test the `Concat` method (Java: `CharSeq.Concat(CharSeq another)`) or `appendAndReturn(CharSequence toAppend)`.

```java
public class CharSeqTests {
    @org.junit.Test
    public void testEmpty() {
        CharSeq cs1 = new CharSeq("");
        CharSeq cs2 = new CharSeq("Test");
        CharSeq result = cs1.Concat(cs2);
        Assert.assertEquals("Test", result.ToString());
    }

    @org.junit.Test
    public void testFirst() {
        CharSeq cs1 = new CharSeq("Hello, ");
        CharSeq cs2 = new CharSeq("World!");
        CharSeq result = cs1.Concat(cs2);
        Assert.assertEquals("Hello, World!", result.ToString());
    }

    @org.junit.Test
    public void testSecond() {
        CharSeq cs1 = new CharSeq("World!");
        CharSeq cs2 = new CharSeq("Hello, ");
        CharSeq result = cs1.Concat(cs2);
        Assert.assertEquals("World! Hello, ", result.ToString());
    }

    @org.junit.Test
    public void testReversed() {
        CharSeq cs1 = new CharSeq("Hello, ");
        CharSeq cs2 = new CharSeq("world!");
        CharSeq reversedCS2 = new CharSeq(new StringBuffer(cs2.ToString()).reverse().toString());
        CharSeq result = cs1.Concat(reversedCS2);
        Assert.assertEquals("Hello, !dlrow ,olleH", result.ToString());
    }

    @org.junit.Test
    public void testNull() {
        CharSeq cs1 = new CharSeq("Hello, ");
        CharSeq result = cs1.Concat(null);
        Assert.assertEquals("Hello, ", result.ToString());
    }
}
```

```csharp
        CharSequence cs2 = new String("Hello, ");
        CharSequence result = appender.appendAndReturn(cs2);
        Assert.AreEqual("World! Hello, ", result.ToString());
    }

    [Test]
    public void testReversed() {
        CharSeqAppender appender = new CharSeqAppender(new StringBuilder("Hello, "));
        CharSequence cs2 = new String(new StringBuilder(cs2.ToString()).Reverse().ToString());
        CharSequence result = appender.appendAndReturn(cs2);
        Assert.AreEqual("Hello, !dlrow ,olleH", result.ToString());
    }

    public void testNull() {

        CharSequence result = appender.appendAndReturn(null);
```

```java
    public void testPropertyChangedEvent() {
        StateNotifier notifier = new StateNotifier();
        Action<String, Integer, Integer> mockListener = mock(Action.class);
        notifier.PropertyChanged += mockListener;

        notifier.SetState(1);
        verify(mockListener).invoke("State", 0, 1);

        notifier.SetState(2);
        verify(mockListener, times(1)).invoke("State", 1, 2);

        notifier.SetState(3);
        verify(mockListener, times(2)).invoke("State", 2, 3);
    }
}
```

```java
import org.junit.Test;
import static org.junit.Assert.*;
import java.util.logging.Level;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;

public class LoggerTest {
    private final DateTimeFormatter dateTimeFormatter = DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("HH:mm:ss");

    @Test
    public void testFormatAndLogThrowable() {
        LocalDateTime now = LocalDateTime.now();
        String msg = "An error occurred";
        Exception throwable = new RuntimeException("Sample exception for logging.");
        Color color = new Color("Red");
        Logger logger = new Logger();

        String expectedFormattedLogLine = "[Error] " + msg;
        String expectedOutput = String.format("[%s] [Red] %s\nException: java.lang.RuntimeException: Sample exception for logging.\n", dateTimeFormatter.format(now), expectedFormattedLogLine);

        logger.FormatAndLogThrowable(Level.ERROR, color, msg, throwable);
        assertEquals(expectedOutput, System.console().reader().readLine());
    }

    @Test
    public void testFormatAndLogThrowableWithoutColors() {
        Logger logger = new Logger();
        logger.USE_COLORS = false;
        LocalDateTime now = LocalDateTime.now();
        String msg = "An error occurred";
        Exception throwable = new RuntimeException("Sample exception for logging.");

        String expectedFormattedLogLine = "[Error] " + msg;
        String expectedOutput = String.format("[%s] [None] %s\nException: java.lang.RuntimeException: Sample exception for logging.\n", dateTimeFormatter.format(now), expectedFormattedLogLine);

        logger.FormatAndLogThrowable(Level.ERROR, null, msg, throwable);
        assertEquals(expectedOutput, System.console().reader().readLine());
    }

    @Test
    public void testFormatAndLogThrowableWithDifferentLevels() {
        LocalDateTime now = LocalDateTime.now();
        String msg = "An error occurred";
        Exception throwable = new RuntimeException("Sample exception for logging.");
        Color color = new Color("Red");
        Logger logger = new Logger();

        // Test with a level that's higher than the current log level
        logger.PhotonLogger.Level = LoggingLevel.Trace;
        String expectedFormattedLogLine = "[Trace] " + msg;
        String expectedOutput = "";

        logger.FormatAndLogThrowable(LoggingLevel.TRACE, color, msg, throwable);
        assertEquals(expectedOutput, System.console().reader().readLine());

        // Test with a level that's equal to the current log level
        logger.PhotonLogger.Level = LoggingLevel.Error;
        expectedFormattedLogLine = "[Error] " + msg;
        expectedOutput = String.format("[%s] [Red] %s\nException: java.lang.RuntimeException: Sample exception for logging.\n", dateTimeFormatter.format(now), expectedFormattedLogLine);

        logger.FormatAndLogThrowable(LoggingLevel.Error, color, msg, throwable);
        assertEquals(expectedOutput, System.console().reader().readLine());
    }
}
```

```java
        manager.printRestrictedDomains();
```

 For both Java and C# programs, I will generate the following unit tests using JUnit for Java and xUnit for C#. The unit tests are designed to verify that the `Acquire()` and `Release()` methods function correctly, as well as ensuring that the read lock can be acquired and released even when a cancellation request is made.

Java:

```java
import org.junit.Assert;
import org.junit.Before;
import org.junit.Test;

public class StoppableReadLockTest {
    private ReadWriteLock rwLock;
    private CancelCriterion cancelCriterion;
    private StoppableReadLock stoppableReadLock;

    @Before
    public void setup() {
        rwLock = new ReadWriteLock();
        cancelCriterion = new CancelCriterion();
        stoppableReadLock = new StoppableReadLock(rwLock, cancelCriterion);
    }

    @Test
    public void testAcquireReleaseNormalCase() {
        stoppableReadLock.Acquire();
        Assert.assertFalse(cancelCriterion.CancelRequested);
        stoppableReadLock.Release();
    }

    @Test
    public void testAcquireReleaseCancelRequested() {
        cancelCriterion.CancelRequested = true;
        try {
            stoppableReadLock.Acquire();
            Assert.fail("OperationCanceledException expected");
        } catch (OperationCanceledException e) {
            // Expected exception
        }
    }

    @Test(expected = OperationCanceledException.class)
    public void testCancelDuringRelease() {
        stoppableReadLock.Acquire();
        cancelCriterion.CancelRequested = true;
        stoppableReadLock.Release();
    }
}
```

C#:

```csharp
using Xunit;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System.Threading.Operations;

public class StoppableReadLockTest
{
    private readonly ReadWriteLock rwLock = new ReadWriteLock();
    private CancelCriterion cancelCriterion;
    private StoppableReadLock stoppableReadLock;

    public StoppableReadLockTest()
    {
        cancelCriterion = new CancelCriterion { CancelRequested = false };
        stoppableReadLock = new StoppableReadLock(rwLock, cancelCriterion);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void TestAcquireReleaseNormalCase()
    {
        stoppableReadLock.Acquire();
        Assert.False(cancelCriterion.CancelRequested);
        stoppableReadLock.Release();
    }

    [Fact]
    public void TestAcquireReleaseCancelRequested()
    {
        cancelCriterion.CancelRequested = true;
        var exceptionThrown = Assert.Throws<OperationCanceledException>(() => stoppableReadLock.Acquire());
        Assert.Equal("Lock acquisition cancelled.", exceptionThrown.Message);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void TestCancelDuringRelease()
    {
        stoppableReadLock.Acquire();
        cancelCriterion.CancelRequested = true;
        Assert.Throws<OperationCanceledException>(() => stoppableReadLock.Release());
    }
}
```

```java
import org.junit.Assert;
import org.junit.Test;

public class CopyUnitTests {
    @Test
    public void testCopy_NullInput_ThrowsArgumentException() {
        try {
            Program.copyByteArrayToStream(null, new ByteArrayOutputStream());
            Assert.fail("Expected ArgumentNullException when input is null");
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
            // Pass
        }
    }

    @Test
    public void testCopy_NullOutput_ThrowsArgumentException() {
        byte[] data = new byte[] {1, 2, 3, 4, 5};
        try {
            Program.copyByteArrayToStream(data, null);
            Assert.fail("Expected ArgumentNullException when output is null");
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
            // Pass
        }
    }

    @Test
    public void testCopy_EmptyData_CopiesNothing() {
        byte[] data = {};
        ByteArrayOutputStream outputStream = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
        Program.copyByteArrayToStream(data, outputStream);
        byte[] result = outputStream.toByteArray();
        Assert.assertEquals("[]", Arrays.toString(result));
    }

    @Test
    public void testCopy_RegularData_CopiesCorrectly() {
        byte[] data = new byte[] {1, 2, 3, 4, 5};
        ByteArrayOutputStream outputStream = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
        Program.copyByteArrayToStream(data, outputStream);
        byte[] result = outputStream.toByteArray();
        Assert.assertEquals("[1, 2, 3, 4, 5]", Arrays.toString(result));
    }

    @Test
    public void testCopy_CloseStream_DoesNotThrowIOException() {
        byte[] data = new byte[] {1, 2, 3, 4, 5};
        ByteArrayOutputStream outputStream = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
        Program.copyByteArrayToStream(data, outputStream);
        outputStream.close();
    }
}
```

Here's the C# unit test:

```csharp
using NUnit.Framework;
using System;
using System.IO;

public class FrameRendererTest
{
    private readonly string FRAME_DATA = "Decoded Frame Data";

    [Test]
    public void TestRenderNativeFrame()
    {
        using (var output = new StringWriter())
        {
            TextWriterConsole.WriteLine(output, Console.Out);
            FrameRenderer renderer = new FrameRenderer();
            renderer.renderNativeFrame();
            Assert.AreEqual("Rendering frame: " + FRAME_DATA, output.ToString());
        }
    }
}

public static class TextWriterConsole
{
    public static void WriteLine(TextWriter writer, ConsoleTarget target)
    {
        target.SetOut(writer);
        target.WriteLine("");
        target.Reset();
    }

    private class ConsoleTarget : IDisposable
    {
        private readonly TextWriter _output;
        public StreamWriter OutStream => (StreamWriter)_output;

        public void SetOut(TextWriter output)
        {
            if (_output != null) throw new ObjectDisposedException(nameof(_output));
            _output = output;
        }

        public void Reset()
        {
            _output?.Dispose();
            _output = null;
        }

        public ConsoleColor ForegroundColor { get; set; } = Console.ForegroundColor;
        public ConsoleColor BackgroundColor { get; set; } = Console.BackgroundColor;
        public int CursorTop { get; set; }
        public int CursorLeft { get; set; }

        public void WriteLine(string value)
        {
            _output?.Write(value);
            _output?.WriteLine();
        }

        public void Dispose()
        {
            _output?.Dispose();
        }
    }
}
```

```java
public class ProgramTest {
    @Test
    public void testEncodeTextUppercase() {
        String msg = "HELLO WORLD";
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        int initialSubmode = 0;
        int[] result = EncodeText(new StringCharSequence(msg), 0, msg.length(), sb, initialSubmode);
        assertEquals("Original: HELLO WORLD\nCodewords: [7, 4, 8, 5, 0, 7, 12, 3, 9, 4, 13, 10, 0, 16]\nDecoded: ????H????L??W??O??R??D",
                "Original: " + msg + "\nCodewords: " + java.util.Arrays.toString(result) + "\nDecoded: " + sb.toString());
    }

    @Test
    public void testEncodeTextLowercase() {
        String msg = "hello world";
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        int initialSubmode = 0;
        int[] result = EncodeText(new StringCharSequence(msg), 0, msg.length(), sb, initialSubmode);
        assertEquals("Original: hello world\nCodewords: [14, 8, 9, 5, 1, 13, 2, 12, 9, 4, 16, 10, 0]\nDecoded: ????h????l??w??o??r??d",
                "Original: " + msg + "\nCodewords: " + java.util.Arrays.toString(result) + "\nDecoded: " + sb.toString());
    }

    @Test
    public void testEncodeTextMixed() {
        String msg = "Hello, World!";
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();

        assertEquals("Original: Hello, World!\nCodewords: [7, 4, 8, 5, 14, 9, 0, 3, 12, 2, 1, 9, 16, 0, 4, 8, 0, 0]\nDecoded: ????h????l??w??o??r??!",

    }

        assertEquals("Original: !Hello, World$\nCodewords: [8, 7, 14, 9, 5, 13, 2, 8, 3, 16]\nDecoded: !???h????l??w??o??$",
                "Original: " + msg + "\nCodewords: " + java.util.Arrays.toString(result) + "\nDecoded: " + sb.toString());
    }
```

```java
import org.junit.Test;
import static org.junit.Assert.*;

public class NcNameValidatorTest {
    private final NcNameValidator validator = new NcNameValidator();

    @Test
    public void testValidNCNames() {
        String[] testCases = {"validName", "_name123", "A", "valid.name", "valid-name"};
        for (String s : testCases) {
            assertTrue(validator.isValidNCName(s));
        }
    }

    @Test
    public void testInvalidNCNames() {
        String[] testCases = {"1invalid", "invalid:name", "with space", "-", null};
        for (String s : testCases) {
            assertFalse(validator.isValidNCName(s));
        }
    }
}
```

```csharp
            {

            }
        }

        [Fact]
        public void TestInvalidNCNames()
        {

            foreach (var s in testCases)

                Assert.False(validator.isValidNCName(s));
            }
```

```java
import org.junit.Test;
import static org.junit.Assert.*;

public class RepeatedFieldBuilderTest {
    @Test
    public void testAddMessage() {
        RepeatedFieldBuilder<MType, BType, IType> builder = new RepeatedFieldBuilder<>();
        MType message = new MType();
        builder.AddMessage(message);
        assertEquals("Messages count: 1", builder.PrintMessages());
    }

    @Test
    public void testMultipleAddMessages() {
        RepeatedFieldBuilder<MType, BType, IType> builder = new RepeatedFieldBuilder<>();
        MType message1 = new MType();
        MType message2 = new MType();
        builder.AddMessage(message1);
        builder.AddMessage(message2);
        assertEquals("Messages count: 2", builder.PrintMessages());
    }

    @Test
    public void testNullMessage() {
        RepeatedFieldBuilder<MType, BType, IType> builder = new RepeatedFieldBuilder<>();
        MType message = null;
        assertThrows(ArgumentNullException.class, () -> builder.AddMessage(message));
    }

    @Test
    public void testEmptyList() {
        RepeatedFieldBuilder<MType, BType, IType> builder = new RepeatedFieldBuilder<>();
        assertEquals("Messages count: 0", builder.PrintMessages());
    }
}
```

```csharp
        list.Add("Apple");
        list.Add("Banana");
        list.Add("Cherry");
        list.Add("Date");
        Assert.Equal("List after appending: [Apple, Banana, Cherry, Date]", OutputHelper.RunProgram(typeof(AppendToList).Assembly.Location));
    }

    [Fact]

            list.Add(element);
        });

        Assert.Equal("List after appending: []", OutputHelper.RunProgram(typeof(AppendToList).Assembly.Location));
    }
```

```java
import org.junit.Test;
import static org.junit.Assert.assertEquals;

public class ServiceManagerTest {
    @Test
    public void testPrintServiceRegistrars() {
        ServiceManager manager = new ServiceManager();
        String[] expected = {"ServiceA", "ServiceB", "ServiceC"};
        manager.PrintServiceRegistrars();
        List<String> actual = new ArrayList<>();
        manager.BuildServiceRegistrar().forEach(reg -> actual.add(reg.Name));
        assertEquals(expected, actual.toArray());
    }

    @Test
    public void testEmptyList() {
        ServiceManager manager = new ServiceManager();
        manager.proxyRegSet.clear();
        manager.PrintServiceRegistrars();
        assertEquals(0, manager.BuildServiceRegistrar().length);
    }

    @Test
    public void testNullInput() {
        ServiceManager manager = new ServiceManager();
        manager.proxyRegSet = null;
        manager.PrintServiceRegistrars();
        assertEquals(0, manager.BuildServiceRegistrar().length);
    }

    @Test
    public void testSingleElementList() {
        ServiceManager manager = new ServiceManager();
        List<ProxyReg> list = new ArrayList<>();
        list.add(new ProxyReg(new ServiceRegistrar("ServiceX")));
        manager.proxyRegSet = list;
        String[] expected = {"ServiceX"};
        manager.PrintServiceRegistrars();
        List<String> actual = new ArrayList<>();
        manager.BuildServiceRegistrar().forEach(reg -> actual.add(reg.Name));
        assertEquals(expected, actual.toArray());
    }

    @Test
    public void testMultipleElementsList() {
        ServiceManager manager = new ServiceManager();
        List<ProxyReg> list = new ArrayList<>();
        list.add(new ProxyReg(new ServiceRegistrar("ServiceX")));
        list.add(new ProxyReg(new ServiceRegistrar("ServiceY")));
        manager.proxyRegSet = list;
        String[] expected = {"ServiceX", "ServiceY"};
        manager.PrintServiceRegistrars();
        List<String> actual = new ArrayList<>();
        manager.BuildServiceRegistrar().forEach(reg -> actual.add(reg.Name));
        assertEquals(expected, actual.toArray());
    }
}
```

 Here are the unit tests for both the Java and C# programs. The unit tests are designed to check the correctness of the `CheckValue` function in the Java program and the `isValidValue` function in the C# program. Java:

```java
using System;

public class Program {
    // ... existing code ...

    private static void TestValues() {
        boolean[] expectedResults = new boolean[]{true, true, false, false, true};
        String[] testValues = {"123", "12", "123a", "456", "999"}; // valid and invalid values

        for (int i = 0; i < expectedResults.Length; i++) {
            try {
                boolean result = CheckValue(testValues[i]);
                if (result != expectedResults[i]) {
                    Console.WriteLine("Test " + (i + 1) + ": Expected " + expectedResults[i] + ", Got " + result);
                }
            } catch (Exception ex) {
                String expectedMessage = i < 2 ? null : testValues[i].Substring(testValues[i].Length - 1);
                if (ex.Message != (expectedMessage != null ? _STR + expectedMessage + _STR : "")) {
                    Console.WriteLine("Test " + (i + 1) + ": Expected message: " + expectedMessage + ", Got: " + ex.Message);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // ... existing main function ...
}
```

C#:

```csharp
using System;

public class ValueChecker {
    public static bool isValidValue(int value) {
        // For demonstration, let's say valid values are between 1 and 100 (inclusive)
        return value >= 1 && value <= 100;
    }

    public static void Main(String[] args) {
        TestValues();
        int[] testValues = new[] { 42, 1, 99, 101, -1 }; // valid and invalid values

        foreach (int value in testValues) {
            string expectedResult = value >= 1 && value <= 100 ? value + " is a valid value." : value + " is not a valid value.";
            Console.WriteLine(expectedResult);
            if (Console.ReadLine() != expectedResult) {
                Console.WriteLine("Test failed: Expected '" + expectedResult + "', Got something else.");
            }
        }
    }

    private static void TestValues() {
        bool[] expectedResults = new[] { true, false, true, false, false }; // valid and invalid values
        int[] testValues = new[] { 42, 1, 99, 101, -1 }; // valid and invalid values

        for (int i = 0; i < expectedResults.Length; i++) {
            bool result = isValidValue(testValues[i]);
            if (result != expectedResults[i]) {
                Console.WriteLine("Test " + (i + 1) + ": Expected " + expectedResults[i] + ", Got " + result);
            }
        }
    }
}
```

Both programs now contain the `TestValues` method which calls the function to be tested with a predefined set of test cases. The output for each test case is compared against an expected result, and if they don't match, it indicates that the test has failed.

 Here are the unit tests for both Java and C# code. I've created a test class named `QueueManagerTest` in each language and added five unit tests that cover different scenarios.

Java:
```java
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import java.util.*;

public class QueueManagerTest {
    private QueueManager queueManager;
    private Server primaryServer1 = new Server("primary1");
    private Server backupServer1 = new Server("backup1");
    private Server backupServerFail2 = new Server("backup_fail2");
    private Server availableServer1 = new Server("available1");
    private Set<Server> excludedServers;

    @BeforeEach
    public void setUp() {
        this.excludedServers = new HashSet<>();
        QueueConnections queueConnections = new QueueConnections();
        Pool pool = new Pool();
        Logger logger = new Logger();
        this.queueManager = new QueueManager(logger, pool, queueConnections);
    }

    @Test
    public void testPrimaryLost_SuccessfulRecovery() {
        queueManager.setPrimary(new QueueConnectionImpl(primaryServer1));
        queueManager.addBackup(new QueueConnectionImpl(backupServer1));
        Assertions.assertEquals(primaryServer1, queueManager.getPrimary());

        queueManager.RecoverPrimary(excludedServers);
        Assertions.assertFalse(excludedServers.contains(primaryServer1));
        Assertions.assertTrue(excludedServers.contains(backupServerFail2));
        Assertions.assertEquals(backupServer1, queueManager.getPrimary());
    }

    @Test
    public void testPrimaryLost_NoBackupAvailable() {
        queueManager.setPrimary(new QueueConnectionImpl(primaryServer1));
        queueManager.addBackup(backupServerFail2);
        Assertions.assertEquals(primaryServer1, queueManager.getPrimary());

        queueManager.RecoverPrimary(excludedServers);
        Assertions.assertFalse(excludedServers.contains(primaryServer1));
        Assertions.assertTrue(excludedServers.contains(backupServerFail2));
        Assertions.assertEquals(null, queueManager.getPrimary());
    }

    @Test
    public void testPrimaryLost_NoExcludedServers() {
        queueManager.setPrimary(new QueueConnectionImpl(primaryServer1));
        queueManager.addBackup(backupServer1);
        Assertions.assertEquals(primaryServer1, queueManager.getPrimary());

        queueManager.RecoverPrimary(excludedServers);
        Assertions.assertFalse(excludedServers.contains(primaryServer1));
        Assertions.assertFalse(excludedServers.contains(backupServer1));
        Assertions.assertEquals(backupServer1, queueManager.getPrimary());
    }

    @Test
    public void testPrimaryLost_AllBackupsFailed() {
        queueManager.setPrimary(new QueueConnectionImpl(primaryServer1));
        queueManager.addBackup(backupServerFail2);
        queueManager.addBackup(new QueueConnectionImpl(backupServer1));
        Assertions.assertEquals(primaryServer1, queueManager.getPrimary());

        queueManager.RecoverPrimary(excludedServers);
        Assertions.assertFalse(excludedServers.contains(primaryServer1));
        Assertions.assertTrue(excludedServers.contains(backupServerFail2));
        Assertions.assertTrue(excludedServers.contains(backupServer1));
        Assertions.assertEquals(null, queueManager.getPrimary());
    }

    @Test
    public void testPrimaryLost_NoAvailableServers() {
        queueManager.setPrimary(new QueueConnectionImpl(primaryServer1));
        queueManager.addBackup(backupServerFail2);
        queueManager.addBackup(backupServer1);
        excludedServers.add(backupServerFail2);
        excludedServers.add(backupServer1);
        Assertions.assertEquals(primaryServer1, queueManager.getPrimary());

        queueManager.RecoverPrimary(excludedServers);
        Assertions.assertFalse(excludedServers.contains(primaryServer1));
        Assertions.assertTrue(excludedServers.contains(backupServerFail2));
        Assertions.assertTrue(excludedServers.contains(backupServer1));
        Assertions.assertEquals(null, queueManager.getPrimary());
    }
}
```

```csharp
using System;
using Microsoft.VisualStudio.TestTools.UnitTesting;
using Moq;

[TestClass]
public class QueueManagerTests {
    private ServerManager manager;
    private Mock<Logger> loggerMock;
    private Pool poolMock;
    private QueueConnections queueConnectionsMock;
    private HashSet<Server> excludedServers;

    [TestInitialize]
    public void SetUp() {
        loggerMock = new Mock<Logger>();
        poolMock = new Pool();
        queueConnectionsMock = new QueueConnections();
        manager = new ServerManager(new List<string>(), new List<string>(), new List<string>());
        manager.logger = loggerMock.Object;
        manager.pool = poolMock.Object;
        manager.queueConnections = queueConnectionsMock;
        excludedServers = new HashSet<Server>();
    }

    [TestMethod]
    public void TestPrimaryLost_SuccessfulRecovery() {
        var primary = new Server("primary1");
        var backup = new Server("backup1");
        queueConnectionsMock.SetPrimary(new QueueConnectionImpl(primary));
        queueConnectionsMock.AddBackup(new QueueConnectionImpl(backup));
        Assert.AreEqual(primary, queueConnectionsMock.GetPrimary());

        manager.RecoverPrimary(excludedServers);
        Assert.IsFalse(excludedServers.Contains(primary));
        Assert.IsTrue(excludedServers.Contains(new Server("backup_fail2")));
        Assert.AreEqual(backup, queueConnectionsMock.GetPrimary());
    }

    [TestMethod]
    public void TestPrimaryLost_NoBackupAvailable() {
        var primary = new Server("primary1");
        var backupFail2 = new Server("backup_fail2");
        queueConnectionsMock.SetPrimary(new QueueConnectionImpl(primary));
        queueConnectionsMock.AddBackup(backupFail2);
        Assert.AreEqual(primary, queueConnectionsMock.GetPrimary());

        manager.RecoverPrimary(excludedServers);
        Assert.IsFalse(excludedServers.Contains(primary));
        Assert.IsTrue(excludedServers.Contains(backupFail2));
        Assert.AreEqual(null, queueConnectionsMock.GetPrimary());
    }

    [TestMethod]
    public void TestPrimaryLost_NoExcludedServers() {
        var primary = new Server("primary1");
        var backup = new Server("backup1");
        queueConnectionsMock.SetPrimary(new QueueConnectionImpl(primary));
        queueConnectionsMock.AddBackup(backup);
        Assert.AreEqual(primary, queueConnectionsMock.GetPrimary());

        manager.RecoverPrimary(excludedServers);
        Assert.IsFalse(excludedServers.Contains(primary));
        Assert.IsFalse(excludedServers.Contains(backup));
        Assert.AreEqual(backup, queueConnectionsMock.GetPrimary());
    }

    [TestMethod]
    public void TestPrimaryLost_AllBackupsFailed() {
        var primary = new Server("primary1");
        var backupFail2 = new Server("backup_fail2");
        var backup = new Server("backup1");
        queueConnectionsMock.SetPrimary(new QueueConnectionImpl(primary));
        queueConnectionsMock.AddBackup(backupFail2);
        queueConnectionsMock.AddBackup(new QueueConnectionImpl(backup));
        Assert.AreEqual(primary, queueConnectionsMock.GetPrimary());

        manager.RecoverPrimary(excludedServers);
        Assert.IsFalse(excludedServers.Contains(primary));
        Assert.IsTrue(excludedServers.Contains(backupFail2));
        Assert.IsTrue(excludedServers.Contains(backup));
        Assert.AreEqual(null, queueConnectionsMock.GetPrimary());
    }

    [TestMethod]
    public void TestPrimaryLost_NoAvailableServers() {
        var primary = new Server("primary1");
        var backupFail2 = new Server("backup_fail2");
        var backup = new Server("backup1");
        queueConnectionsMock.SetPrimary(new QueueConnectionImpl(primary));
        queueConnectionsMock.AddBackup(backupFail2);
        queueConnectionsMock.AddBackup(backup);
        excludedServers.Add(backupFail2);
        excludedServers.Add(backup);
        Assert.AreEqual(primary, queueConnectionsMock.GetPrimary());

        manager.RecoverPrimary(excludedServers);
        Assert.IsFalse(excludedServers.Contains(primary));
        Assert.IsTrue(excludedServers.Contains(backupFail2));
        Assert.IsTrue(excludedServers.Contains(backup));
        Assert.AreEqual(null, queueConnectionsMock.GetPrimary());
    }
}
```

 For the given Java and C# programs, I will generate unit tests for both languages that check the functionality of omitting redundant information from a journal. The unit tests are as follows:

Java:
```java
import org.junit.Assert;
import org.junit.Test;
import JournalRebuilder.*;

public class TestJournal {
    @Test
    public void testOmitRedundantInformation() {
        Journal journal = new Journal();
        journal.addEntry("Started project");
        journal.addEntry("Added feature A");
        journal.addEntry("Added feature A"); // redundant
        journal.addEntry("Fixed bug");
        journal.addEntry("Fixed bug"); // redundant
        journal.addEntry("Refactored code");
        journal.addEntry("Deployed application");

        Journal newJournal = journal.omitRedundantInformation();
        Assert.assertEquals(6, newJournal.getEntries().size());
        Assert.assertFalse(newJournal.getEntries().contains("Added feature A"));
        Assert.assertFalse(newJournal.getEntries().contains("Fixed bug"));
    }
}
```

C#:
```csharp

        currentJournal.addEntry("Started project");
        currentJournal.addEntry("Added feature A");
        currentJournal.addEntry("Added feature A"); // redundant
        currentJournal.addEntry("Fixed bug");
        currentJournal.addEntry("Fixed bug"); // redundant
        currentJournal.addEntry("Refactored code");
        currentJournal.addEntry("Deployed application");

        Journal newJournal = currentJournal.omitRedundantInformation();
        Assert.AreEqual(6, newJournal.getEntries().Count);
        Assert.IsFalse(newJournal.getEntries().Contains("Added feature A"));

    }
}
```

 Here are the unit tests for both Java and C# programs that simulate removing a specific recurrence rule and check if it's successfully removed, as well as handling exceptions in case of errors:

Java:

```java
import org.junit.jupiter.api.*;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.stream.Stream;

public class RecurrenceHandlerTest {
    private List<RecurrenceRule> rRulesList;
    private List<RecurrenceRule> eRulesList;
    private Info info;
    private RecurrenceHandler recurrenceHandler;

    @BeforeEach
    public void setup() {
        rRulesList = new ArrayList<>();
        eRulesList = new ArrayList<>();
        info = new Info();
        rRulesList.add(new RecurrenceRule());
        eRulesList.add(new RecurrenceRule());
        recurrenceHandler = new RecurrenceHandler(rRulesList, eRulesList, info);
    }

    @Test
    public void testRemoveSuccess() {
        recurrenceHandler.Remove();
        assertEquals("RecurrenceRule removed.", recurrenceHandler.rRulesList.get(0).toString());
        assertTrue(recurrenceHandler.eRulesList.isEmpty());
        assertFalse(info.ToString().equals("Info removed."));
    }

    @Test
    public void testRemoveWithError() {
        List<RecurrenceRule> rulesList = new ArrayList<>();
        rulesList.add(new RecurrenceRule());
        rulesList.get(0).Remove(); // Simulate an error
        Assertions.assertThrows(RecurrenceInfoException.class, () -> recurrenceHandler.Remove());
    }

    @Test
    public void testRemoveEmptyList() {
        recurrenceHandler = new RecurrenceHandler(new ArrayList<>(), new ArrayList<>(), info);
        Assertions.assertThrows(RecurrenceInfoException.class, () -> recurrenceHandler.Remove());
    }
}
```

C#:

```csharp
using System;
using Microsoft.VisualStudio.TestTools.UnitTesting;
using System.Collections.Generic;

[TestClass]
public class RuleManagerTests {
    private List<string> rules;
    private RuleManager ruleManager;

    [TestInitialize]
    public void Setup() {
        rules = new List<string>() { "Rule 1: No running", "Rule 2: No shouting", "Recurrence: Water plants every Sunday" };
        ruleManager = new RuleManager();
        foreach (var rule in rules) {
            ruleManager.addRule(rule);
        }
    }

    [TestMethod]
    public void TestRemoveSuccess() {
        ruleManager.removeLastRule();
        Assert.AreEqual("Current rules in persistent store:", Console.Out.ToString().Substring(0, 29));
        Assert.AreNotEqual("Recurrence: Water plants every Sunday", Console.Out.ToString().Substring(Console.Out.ToString().IndexOf("Current rules in persistent store:\n")));
    }

    [TestMethod]
    public void TestRemoveWithError() {
        ruleManager.rules[1] = "Rule 2: No error shouting"; // Simulate an error
        Assert.ThrowsException<System.Exception>(() => ruleManager.removeLastRule());
    }

    [TestMethod]
    public void TestRemoveEmptyList() {
        RuleManager emptyRuleManager = new RuleManager();
        Assert.ThrowsException<System.Exception>(() => emptyRuleManager.removeLastRule());
    }
}
```

```java
        sampleNodes.add(null);
        sampleNodes.add(new SimpleNode("B"));
        sampleNodes.add(new SimpleNode("C"));
        sampleNodes.add(null);

        NodeSetDTM nodeSet = new NodeSetDTM();
        DTMIterator iterator = new SimpleDTMIterator(sampleNodes);
        XPathContext context = new XPathContext();

        nodeSet.AddNodesInDocOrder(iterator, context);
        assertTrue(TestNodeList.compare(nodeSet.nodes, sampleNodes));
    }
}
```

For the TestNodeList class in C#:

```csharp
public static class TestNodeList
{
    public static bool Compare(SimpleNodeList first, SimpleNodeList second)
    {
        if (first.Count != second.Count) return false;
        for (int i = 0; i < first.Count; i++)
        {
            if (!(first[i].GetType() == second[i].GetType()) || !first[i].ToString().Equals(second[i].ToString()))
                return false;
        }
        return true;
    }
}
```

Java:
```java
import org.junit.jupiter.api.*;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

public class DataFetcherTest {
    private DataFetcher fetcher;
    private long startTime;

    @BeforeEach
    void setup() {
        // Arrange - Initialize the DataFetcher instance and record the current time
        fetcher = new DataFetcher(2000);
        startTime = System.currentTimeMillis();
    }

    @Test
    void testDataValidInitially() {
        // Act - Check if data is initially valid (should be true)
        assertTrue(fetcher.isDataValid());
    }

    @Test
    void testDataValidLessThanInterval() {
        // Act - Wait less than the fetch interval and check if data is valid (should be true)
        long sleepTime = 1500;
        System.out.println("Sleeping for " + sleepTime + "ms");
        try {
            Thread.sleep(sleepTime);
        } catch (InterruptedException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
        }
        long currentTime = System.currentTimeMillis();
        assertTrue(fetcher.isDataValid()); // Assert - Check if data is still valid
    }

    @Test
    void testDataValidEqualToInterval() {
        // Act - Wait exactly the fetch interval and check if data is valid (should be true)
        long sleepTime = 2001;
        System.out.println("Sleeping for " + sleepTime + "ms");
        try {
            Thread.sleep(sleepTime);
        } catch (InterruptedException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
        }
        long currentTime = System.currentTimeMillis();
        assertTrue(fetcher.isDataValid()); // Assert - Check if data is still valid
    }

    @Test
    void testDataValidMoreThanIntervalButLessThanDouble() {
        // Act - Wait more than the fetch interval but less than double the fetch interval and check if data is valid (should be true)
        long sleepTime = 4010;
        System.out.println("Sleeping for " + sleepTime + "ms");
        try {
            Thread.sleep(sleepTime);
        } catch (InterruptedException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
        }
        long currentTime = System.currentTimeMillis();
        assertTrue(fetcher.isDataValid()); // Assert - Check if data is still valid
    }

    @Test
    void testDataValidMoreThanDoubleInterval() {
        // Act - Wait more than double the fetch interval and check if data is fetched (should be true)
        long sleepTime = 40010;
        System.out.println("Sleeping for " + sleepTime + "ms");
        try {
            Thread.sleep(sleepTime);
        } catch (InterruptedException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
        }
        long currentTime = System.currentTimeMillis();
        assertTrue(fetcher.isDataValid()); // Assert - Check if data is fetched
    }
}
```

Java Unit Tests:

```java
import static org.junit.Assert.*;
import org.junit.Test;

public class MyHandlerTest {
    @Test
    public void testHandleRequest() {
        MyArgument argument = new MyArgument();
        RequestContext requestContext = new RequestContext();
        MyHandler handler = new MyHandler();
        handler.HandleRequest(requestContext);
        String[] expectedOutput = {"Agent: agent1, Type: type1", "Agent: agent3, Type: type1"};
        assertEquals(expectedOutput, handler.getOutput());
    }

    @Test
    public void testHandleRequest_NoFilter() {
        MyArgument argument = new MyArgument();
        RequestContext requestContext = new RequestContext();
        argument.FILTER = null;
        MyHandler handler = new MyHandler();
        handler.HandleRequest(requestContext);
        String[] expectedOutput = {"Agent: agent1, Type: type1", "Agent: agent3, Type: type1"};
        assertEquals(expectedOutput, handler.getOutput());
    }

    @Test
    public void testHandleRequest_EmptyFilter() {
        MyArgument argument = new MyArgument();
        RequestContext requestContext = new RequestContext();
        argument.FILTER = "";
        MyHandler handler = new MyHandler();
        handler.HandleRequest(requestContext);
        String[] expectedOutput = {"Agent: agent1, Type: type1", "Agent: agent3, Type: type1"};
        assertEquals(expectedOutput, handler.getOutput());
    }

    @Test
    public void testHandleRequest_NoMatchingAgents() {
        MyArgument argument = new MyArgument();
        RequestContext requestContext = new RequestContext();
        List<AMIdentity> identities = new ArrayList<>();
        AMIdentityRepository amir = new AMIdentityRepository(new SSOToken(), "myRealm");
        IdSearchResults isr = amir.SearchIdentities(IdType.AGENTONLY, "", new IdSearchControl());
        identities.addAll(isr.GetSearchResults());
        identities.removeIf(am -> !am.Type.equals("type1"));
        MyHandler handler = new MyHandler();
        handler.HandleRequest(requestContext);
        String[] expectedOutput = {"No matching agents found."};
        assertEquals(expectedOutput, handler.getOutput());
    }

    @Test
    public void testHandleRequest_Exception() {
        Exception exception = new RuntimeException("Expected exception");
        MyArgument argument = new MyArgument();
        RequestContext requestContext = new RequestContext();
        AMIdentityRepository amir = new AMIdentityRepository(new SSOToken(), "myRealm");
        IdSearchResults isr = amir.SearchIdentities(IdType.AGENTONLY, "", new IdSearchControl());
        when(isr.GetSearchResults()).thenThrow(exception);
        MyHandler handler = new MyHandler();
        handler.HandleRequest(requestContext);
        String[] expectedOutput = {"Error: Expected exception"};
        assertEquals(expectedOutput, handler.getOutput());
    }
}
```

Java:
```java
import org.junit.Assert.*;
import org.junit.Test;

public class NestedObjectTest {
    @Test
    public void testSerializeNestedObject() throws IOException {
        // Given a nested object instance
        NestedObject obj = new NestedObject();

        // When serialize it to bytes
        byte[] serializedBytes = obj.ToBytes();

        // Then the length of the serialized bytes should be 5 (indicating that 5 bytes were written)
        assertEquals(5, serializedBytes.length);

        // Given a new nested object instance with the same data
        NestedObject obj2 = new NestedObject();
        byte[] deserializedBytes = obj2.ToBytes();

        // When compare the serialized and deserialized bytes
        Assert.assertArrayEquals(serializedBytes, deserializedBytes);
    }
}
```

C#:
```csharp
using Microsoft.VisualStudio.TestTools.UnitTesting;
using System;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;

[TestClass]
public class ProgramTests {
    [TestMethod]
    public void TestSerializeNestedObject() {
        // Given a nested object instance
        var obj = new NestedObject();

        // When serialize it to bytes
        var serializedBytes = obj.ToBytes().ToArray();

        // Then the length of the serialized bytes should be 5 (indicating that 5 bytes were written)
        Assert.AreEqual(5, serializedBytes.Length);

        // Given a new nested object instance with the same data
        var obj2 = new NestedObject();
        var deserializedBytes = obj2.ToBytes().ToArray();

        // When compare the serialized and deserialized bytes
        Assert.AreEqual(serializedBytes, deserializedBytes);
    }
}
```

 Here are the unit tests for both Java and C# programs. I have created a test class called `ExportMaskTest` in both languages. The tests check if the correct ExportMasks are returned when given different sets of URIs.

Java:

```java
import org.junit.Assert;
import org.junit.Test;

public class ExportMaskTest {
    private DummyDbClient dbClient;

    @Test
    public void testGetExportMasks() {
        // Setup sample export masks
        var mask1Uri = new Uri("urn:mask:1");
        var mask2Uri = new Uri("urn:mask:2");
        var mask3Uri = new Uri("urn:mask:3");

        var allMasks = new Dictionary<Uri, ExportMask> {
            { mask1Uri, new ExportMask(mask1Uri) },
            { mask2Uri, new ExportMask(mask2Uri) },
            { mask3Uri, new ExportMask(mask3Uri) }
        };

        dbClient = new DummyDbClient(allMasks);

        // Test case 1: All masks are present
        var allMasksMap = CreateExportMaskMap(new HashSet<>(allMasks.Keys));
        Assert.assertEquals(allMasks.Count, allMasksMap.Count);
        for (var kv : allMasks) {
            Assert.assertTrue(allMasksMap.ContainsKey(kv.Key));
            Assert.assertEquals(kv.Value, allMasksMap[kv.Key]);
        }

        // Test case 2: Only some masks are present
        var placedMasks = new HashSet<Uri> { mask1Uri, mask3Uri };
        var placedMasksMap = CreateExportMaskMap(placedMasks);
        Assert.assertEquals(placedMasks.Count, placedMasksMap.Count);
        for (var kv : placedMasks) {
            Assert.assertTrue(placedMasksMap.ContainsKey(kv));
            Assert.assertEquals(kv, placedMasksMap[kv]);
        }
    }
}
```

```csharp
public class ExportMaskTest

    private static Map<string, ExportMask> exportMaskStore;

    [SetUp]
    public void Setup()
    {
        // Simulate a database or storage of ExportMasks
        exportMaskStore = new Dictionary<string, ExportMask>();
        exportMaskStore.Add("mask1", new ExportMask("mask1", "Data1"));
        exportMaskStore.Add("mask2", new ExportMask("mask2", "Data2"));
        exportMaskStore.Add("mask3", new ExportMask("mask3", "Data3"));
    }

    [Test]
    public void TestGetExportMasks()
    {
        // Given a set of exportmask URIs, return a map of exportmask URI to ExportMask object
        Set<string> uris = new HashSet<string>();
        uris.Add("mask1");
        uris.Add("mask3");
        var maskMap = getExportMasksByUris(uris);

        // Test case 1: All masks are present
        Assert.AreEqual(exportMaskStore.Count, maskMap.Count);
        foreach (var kv in exportMaskStore)
        {
            Assert.IsTrue(maskMap.ContainsKey(kv.Key));
            Assert.AreEqual(kv.Value, maskMap[kv.Key]);
        }

        // Test case 2: Only some masks are present
        var placedUris = new HashSet<string> { "mask1", "mask3" };
        var placedMasksMap = getExportMasksByUris(placedUris);
        Assert.AreEqual(placedUris.Count, placedMasksMap.Count);
        foreach (var kv in placedUris)
        {
            Assert.IsTrue(placedMasksMap.ContainsKey(kv));
            Assert.AreEqual(kv, placedMasksMap[kv]);
```

 Here are the unit tests for both the Java and C# programs. The unit tests are designed to test the Insert method for various scenarios, including an empty list, inserting at the beginning, inserting at the end, inserting in the middle, and testing the edge case where the collection is not extendable.

Java:

```java
import org.junit.Test;
import static org.junit.Assert.*;

public class ImmutableArray2Test {
    @Test
    public void testEmptyList() {
        ImmutableArray2<Integer> arr = new ImmutableArray2<>(new Integer[0], 0, false);
        assertArrayEquals(new Integer[0], arr.Insert(0, 1).array);
    }

    @Test
    public void testInsertAtBeginning() {
        var initial = new int[]{1, 2, 3};
        ImmutableArray2<Integer> arr = new ImmutableArray2<>(initial, initial.length, false);
        ImmutableArray2<Integer> arr2 = arr.Insert(0, 0);
        assertArrayEquals(new int[]{0, 1, 2, 3}, arr2.array);
    }

    @Test
    public void testInsertAtEnd() {
        var initial = new int[]{1, 2, 3};
        ImmutableArray2<Integer> arr = new ImmutableArray2<>(initial, initial.length, false);
        ImmutableArray2<Integer> arr2 = arr.Insert(4, 4);
        assertArrayEquals(new int[]{1, 2, 3, 4}, arr2.array);
    }

    @Test
    public void testInsertInMiddle() {
        var initial = new int[]{1, 2, 3};
        ImmutableArray2<Integer> arr = new ImmutableArray2<>(initial, initial.length, false);
        ImmutableArray2<Integer> arr2 = arr.Insert(2, 4);
        assertArrayEquals(new int[]{1, 2, 4, 3}, arr2.array);
    }

    @Test
    public void testNotExtendable() {
        ImmutableArray2<Integer> arr = new ImmutableArray2<>(new Integer[0], 0, false);
        assertArrayEquals(new Integer[0], arr.Insert(0, 1).array);
        ImmutableArray2<Integer> arr2 = new ImmutableArray2<>(arr.array, arr.Length, true);
        ImmutableArray2<Integer> arr3 = arr2.Insert(0, 1);
        assertArrayEquals(new int[]{1}, arr3.array);
    }
}
```

```csharp
using System;

public class InsertArrayIntoVectorTest

    private Vector<int[]> vector;
    [SetUp]
    public void Setup()
    {
        var array1 = new int[] { 1, 2, 3 };
        var array2 = new int[] { 4, 5, 6 };
        this.vector = new Vector<int[]>();
        vector.Add(array1);
    }

    [Test]
    public void TestEmptyList()
    {
        Vector<int[]> arr = new Vector<int[]>();
        var arr2 = arr.InsertElementAt(new int[0], 0);
        Assert.AreEqual(0, arr2.Count);
        foreach (var item in arr2)
            Assert.AreEqual(0, item.Length);
    }

    [Test]
    public void TestInsertAtBeginning()
    {
        var arr2 = vector.InsertElementAt(new int[] { 0 }, 0);
        Assert.AreEqual(1, arr2.Count);
        Assert.AreEqual(arr[0].Length + 1, arr2[0].Length);
        Assert.AreEqual(0, arr2[0][0]);
    }

    [Test]
    public void TestInsertAtEnd()
    {
        var arr2 = vector.InsertElementAt(new int[] { 4 }, vector.Count);
        Assert.AreEqual(1, vector.Count);
        Assert.AreEqual(arr[0].Length + 1, arr2[0].Length);
        Assert.AreEqual(arr[0][arr[0].Length - 1], arr2[0][arr2[0].Length - 2]);
        Assert.AreEqual(4, arr2[0][arr2[0].Length - 1]);
    }

    [Test]
    public void TestInsertInMiddle()
    {
        var arr2 = vector.InsertElementAt(new int[] { 4 }, 1);
        Assert.AreEqual(1, arr2.Count);
        Assert.AreEqual(arr[0].Length + 1, arr2[0].Length);
        Assert.AreEqual(arr[0][0], arr2[0][0]);
        Assert.AreEqual(4, arr2[0][1]);
    }
```

CODE COUNT: 82

 For both Java and C# programs, I'll provide the following five unit tests to verify the functionality of putting a root tree node into a cache. The unit tests will ensure that the cache stores the correct root nodes for given document IDs, and also handle cases where duplicate insertions are attempted with the same document ID.

Java:

```java
class Program {
    static void Main(String[] args) {
        DocumentCache cache = new DocumentCache();

        SourceTree doc1Root = new SourceTree(1, "doc1.xml");
        SourceTree duplicatedDoc1Root = new SourceTree(1, "doc1.xml");

        SourceTree doc2Root = new SourceTree(2, "doc2.xml");
        SourceTree duplicatedDoc2Root = new SourceTree(2, "doc2.xml");

        // Test 1: Correct insertion of root node into cache
        cache.PutDocumentInCache(doc1Root.Node, doc1Root);
        assert CacheContainsCorrectlyInsertedRootNode("doc1.xml", doc1Root, cache);

        // Test 2: Duplicate insertion with the same document ID should not throw an exception
        cache.PutDocumentInCache(duplicatedDoc1Root.Node, duplicatedDoc1Root);
        assert CacheContainsCorrectlyInsertedRootNode("doc1.xml", duplicatedDoc1Root, cache);

        // Test 3: Correct insertion of another root node into cache
        cache.PutDocumentInCache(doc2Root.Node, doc2Root);
        assert CacheContainsCorrectlyInsertedRootNode("doc2.xml", doc2Root, cache);

        // Test 4: Duplicate insertion with the same document ID should not replace the existing root node in the cache
        cache.PutDocumentInCache(duplicatedDoc2Root.Node, duplicatedDoc2Root);
        assert CacheContainsCorrectlyInsertedRootNode("doc2.xml", doc2Root, cache);

        // Test 5: Check that an incorrect document ID results in an empty root node
        SourceTree invalidDocIdRoot = new SourceTree(0, "invalid_doc_id.xml");
        assert CacheContainsEmptyRootNode("invalid_doc_id.xml", invalidDocIdRoot, cache);
    }

    private static void assert(boolean condition, String message) {
        if (!condition) {
            throw new RuntimeException(message);
        }
    }

    private static boolean CacheContainsCorrectlyInsertedRootNode(String expectedSystemId, SourceTree expectedSourceTree, DocumentCache cache) {
        TreeNode retrievedRoot = (TreeNode)cache.m_sourceTree.Find((item) -> item.SystemId.equals(expectedSystemId));
        return ((retrievedRoot != null && retrievedRoot.GetValue() == expectedSourceTree.Node) && (retrievedRoot.left == null && retrievedRoot.right == null));
    }

    private static boolean CacheContainsEmptyRootNode(String expectedSystemId, SourceTree expectedSourceTree, DocumentCache cache) {
        TreeNode retrievedRoot = (TreeNode)cache.m_sourceTree.Find((item) -> item.SystemId.equals(expectedSystemId));
        return ((retrievedRoot == null || retrievedRoot == null) && (expectedSourceTree != null && expectedSourceTree.Node == 0));
    }
}
```

```csharp
using System;
using NUnit.Framework;
using static DocumentCacheExample.DocumentCache;

namespace TestDocumentCacheExample
{
    public class Tests
    {
        [SetUp]
        public void Setup()
        {
            // Reset cache between tests for clean state
            nodeCache = new Dictionary<string, int>();
            m_sourceTree = new List<SourceTree>();
        }

        [Test]
        public void TestCorrectInsertionOfRootNodeIntoCache()
        {
            SourceTree doc1Root = new SourceTree(1, "doc1.xml");
            PutDocumentInCache(doc1Root.Node, doc1Root);
            Assert.IsTrue(CacheContainsCorrectlyInsertedRootNode("doc1.xml", doc1Root));
        }

        [Test]
        public void TestDuplicateInsertionWithTheSameDocumentID()
        {
            SourceTree duplicatedDoc1Root = new SourceTree(1, "doc1.xml");
            PutDocumentInCache(duplicatedDoc1Root.Node, duplicatedDoc1Root);
            Assert.IsTrue(CacheContainsCorrectlyInsertedRootNode("doc1.xml", duplicatedDoc1Root));
        }

        [Test]
        public void TestCorrectInsertionOfAnotherRootNodeIntoCache()
        {
            SourceTree doc2Root = new SourceTree(2, "doc2.xml");
            PutDocumentInCache(doc2Root.Node, doc2Root);
            Assert.IsTrue(CacheContainsCorrectlyInsertedRootNode("doc2.xml", doc2Root));
        }

        [Test]
        public void TestDuplicateInsertionWithTheSameDocumentIDDoesNotReplaceExistingRootNode()
        {
            SourceTree duplicatedDoc2Root = new SourceTree(2, "doc2.xml");
            PutDocumentInCache(duplicatedDoc2Root.Node, duplicatedDoc2Root);
            Assert.IsTrue(CacheContainsCorrectlyInsertedRootNode("doc2.xml", duplicatedDoc2Root));
        }

        [Test]
        public void TestCheckThatAnIncorrectDocumentIDResultsInAnEmptyRootNode()
        {
            SourceTree invalidDocIdRoot = new SourceTree(0, "invalid_doc_id.xml");
            Assert.IsTrue(CacheContainsEmptyRootNode("invalid_doc_id.xml", invalidDocIdRoot));
        }

        private bool CacheContainsCorrectlyInsertedRootNode(string expectedSystemId, SourceTree expectedSourceTree)
        {
            TreeNode retrievedRoot = m_sourceTree.Find((item) => item.SystemId == expectedSystemId);
            return (retrievedRoot != null && retrievedRoot.Node == expectedSourceTree.Node && retrievedRoot.left == null && retrievedRoot.right == null);
        }

        private bool CacheContainsEmptyRootNode(SourceTree expectedSourceTree)
        {
            TreeNode retrievedRoot = m_sourceTree.Find((item) => item.SystemId == expectedSourceTree.SystemId);
            return (retrievedRoot == null || retrievedRoot == null && expectedSourceTree != null && expectedSourceTree.Node == 0);
        }
    }
}
```

 Here are the unit tests for both Java and C# programs. I've created an abstract `ITestable` interface for both languages to keep the tests consistent.

Java:

```java
public interface ITestable {
    void test1();
    void test2();
    void test3();
    void test4();
    void test5();
}

public class PluginTest implements ITestable {

    private MainHost host;
    private Plugin plugin;

    public PluginTest() {
        host = new MainHost();
        plugin = new Plugin(host);
    }

    @Override
    public void test1() {
        plugin.RunTask();
        assertEquals("Progress: Initializing [0%]", host.getLastMessage());
    }

    @Override
    public void test2() {
        plugin.RunTask();
        assertEquals("Progress: Loading Data [10%]", host.getLastMessage());
        plugin.RunTask(); // Should not trigger an update
        assertEquals("Progress: Processing [50%]", host.getLastMessage()); // Should not trigger an update
    }

    @Override
    public void test3() {
        plugin.RunTask();
        plugin.RunTask();
        assertEquals("Progress: Loading Data [10%]", host.getLastMessage()); // Should not trigger an update
        plugin.RunTask();
        assertEquals("Progress: Processing [50%]", host.getHost().getLastMessage()); // Should not trigger an update
        plugin.RunTask(); // Update should occur
        assertEquals("Progress: Finalizing [100%]", host.getHost().getLastMessage());
    }

    @Override
    public void test4() {
        plugin.UpdateProgress("Custom Progress Label", 25);
        assertEquals("Progress: Custom Progress Label [25%]", host.getLastMessage());
    }

    @Override
    public void test5() {
        // Test null host scenario
        Plugin pluginNullHost = new Plugin(null);
        pluginNullHost.RunTask();
        assertEquals(null, host.getLastMessage());
    }
}
```

```csharp
public interface ITestable
{
    void Test1();
    void Test2();
    void Test3();
    void Test4();
    void Test5();
}

public class ProgressUpdateEventTest : ITestable
{
    private static readonly ProgressUpdateEvent[] updates = {
        new ProgressUpdateEvent(25, "Initializing data..."),
        new ProgressUpdateEvent(50, "Processing records..."),
        new ProgressUpdateEvent(75, "Applying calculations..."),
        new ProgressUpdateEvent(100, "Complete!")
    };

    private static ProgressUpdateEvent[] remainingUpdates;

    public ProgressUpdateEventTest()
    {
        remainingUpdates = updates.ToArray();
    }

    public void Test1()
    {
        foreach (var update in updates)
        {
            Console.WriteLine(update);
        }
        assertEquals("Progress: Initializing [0%] - Initializing data...", remainingUpdates[0].ToString());
    }

    public void Test2()
    {
        foreach (var update in updates)
        {
            Console.WriteLine(update);
        }
        assertEquals("Progress: Loading Data [10%]", remainingUpdates[1].ToString());
        var currentUpdate = remainingUpdates[1];
        // Simulate multiple calls to RunTask without a new progress update
        foreach (var _ in Enumerable.Range(2, 2))
        {
            Console.WriteLine(currentUpdate);
            remainingUpdates = remainingUpdates.Skip(1).ToArray();
        }
        assertEquals("Progress: Processing [50%]", remainingUpdates[0].ToString()); // Should not trigger an update
    }

    public void Test3()
    {
        foreach (var update in updates)
        {
            Console.WriteLine(update);
        }
        var currentUpdate = remainingUpdates[1];
        // Simulate multiple calls to RunTask without a new progress update
        for (int i = 2; i < 4; i++)
        {
            Console.WriteLine(currentUpdate);
            remainingUpdates = remainingUpdates.Skip(1).ToArray();
        }
        assertEquals("Progress: Loading Data [10%]", remainingUpdates[0].ToString()); // Should not trigger an update
        // Add a new progress update
        remainingUpdates[1] = new ProgressUpdateEvent(52, "Processing records...");
        Console.WriteLine(remainingUpdates[1]);
        remainingUpdates = remainingUpdates.Skip(1).ToArray();
        assertEquals("Progress: Processing [50%]", remainingUpdates[0].ToString()); // Should not trigger an update
        // Add another new progress update
        Console.WriteLine(remainingUpdates[0]);
        remainingUpdates = remainingUpdates.Skip(1).ToArray();
        assertEquals("Progress: Finalizing [100%]", remainingUpdates[0].ToString());
    }

    public void Test4()
    {
        var update = new ProgressUpdateEvent(25, "Custom Progress Label");
        Console.WriteLine(update);
        assertEquals("Progress: Custom Progress Label [25%]", update.ToString());
    }

    public void Test5()
    {
        // Test null scenario
        Action action = () => {
            foreach (var update in updates)
            {
                Console.WriteLine(update);
            }
        };
        Assert.Throws<NullReferenceException>(action);
    }
}
```

```java
import org.junit.Assert;
import org.junit.Test;
import java.io.*;
import static org.junit.Assert.*;

public class ProgramTest {
    @Test
    public void testCopy() throws IOException {
        String inputFile = "testInput.txt";
        String outputFile = "testOutput.txt";

        File.createNewFile(inputFile);
        FileWriter fw = new FileWriter(inputFile);
        fw.write("Hello, Resource Copy!");
        fw.close();

        Resource inputResource = new Resource(inputFile);
        Resource outputResource = new Resource(outputFile);

        using (InputStream inputStream = inputResource.GetInputStream()) {
            Program.Copy(inputStream, outputResource, true);
        }

        String expectedOutput = "Hello, Resource Copy!";
        String actualOutput = new String( Files.readAllBytes(Paths.get(outputFile)) );
        Assert.assertEquals("File copied correctly", expectedOutput, actualOutput);
    }

    @Test
    public void testCloseEL() throws IOException {
        ByteArrayInputStream inputStream = new ByteArrayInputStream("test data".getBytes());
        ByteArrayOutputStream outputStream = new ByteArrayOutputStream();

        Program.Copy(inputStream, new Resource("testOutput.txt"), true);
        Program.IOUtil.CloseEL(inputStream);

        Assert.assertThrows("IOException should be thrown when closing input stream", IOException.class, () -> Program.IOUtil.CloseEL(inputStream));
    }

    @Test
    public void testCopyWithClose() throws IOException {
        String inputFile = "testInput.txt";
        String outputFile = "testOutput.txt";

        File.createNewFile(inputFile);
        FileWriter fw = new FileWriter(inputFile);
        fw.write("Hello, Resource Copy!");
        fw.close();

        Resource inputResource = new Resource(inputFile);
        Resource outputResource = new Resource(outputFile);

        Program.Copy(inputResource.GetInputStream(), outputResource, true, true);

        String expectedOutput = "Hello, Resource Copy!";
        String actualOutput = new String( Files.readAllBytes(Paths.get(outputFile)) );
        Assert.assertEquals("File copied and closed correctly", expectedOutput, actualOutput);
    }

    @Test
    public void testCopyWithoutClose() throws IOException {
        String inputFile = "testInput.txt";
        String outputFile = "testOutput.txt";

        File.createNewFile(inputFile);
        FileWriter fw = new FileWriter(inputFile);
        fw.write("Hello, Resource Copy!");
        fw.close();

        Resource inputResource = new Resource(inputFile);
        Resource outputResource = new Resource(outputFile);

        Program.Copy(inputResource.GetInputStream(), outputResource, false, true);

        String expectedOutput = "Hello, Resource Copy!";
        String actualOutput = new String( Files.readAllBytes(Paths.get(outputFile)) );
        Assert.assertEquals("File copied without closing correctly", expectedOutput, actualOutput);
    }

    @Test
    public void testBufferedOutputStream() throws IOException {
        ByteArrayInputStream inputStream = new ByteArrayInputStream("test data".getBytes());
        ByteArrayOutputStream outputStream = Program.Program.ToBufferedOutputStream(new ByteArrayOutputStream());

        Program.Copy(inputStream, outputStream, true, true);

        String expectedOutput = "test data";
        String actualOutput = new String(outputStream.toByteArray());
        Assert.assertEquals("Buffer output stream correctly", expectedOutput, actualOutput);
    }
}
```

```java
import org.junit.Test;
import static org.junit.Assert.*;

public class ProgramTest {
    @Test
    public void testLoadProperties() {
        Properties props = new Properties();
        Type[] classTypes = { ExampleClass1.class, ExampleClass2.class };
        boolean inherit = true;
        ClassProperties cp = Program.LoadProperties(classTypes, props, inherit);

        // Test for ExampleClass1 when platform is Windows
        cp.Load(ExampleClass1.class.getClassType(), true);
        assertEquals("C:\\Program Files\\App", cp.getPropertyValue("path"));

        // Test for ExampleClass2 when platform is Windows
        cp.Load(ExampleClass2.class.getClassType(), true);
        assertEquals(null, cp.getPropertyValue("path"));

        // Test for ExampleClass1 when platform is Linux
        cp.Load(ExampleClass1.class.getClassType(), false);
        assertEquals("/usr/bin/app", cp.getPropertyValue("path"));

        // Test for ExampleClass2 when platform is Linux
        cp.Load(ExampleClass2.class.getClassType(), false);
        assertEquals(null, cp.getPropertyValue("path"));
    }
}
```

```java
import static org.junit.Assert.*;
import java.util.List;

public class ProgramTest {
    @org.junit.Test
    public void fixGPSTimeStampTest() {
        XMPNode exifSchema = new XMPNode("exif", null);
        exifSchema.AddChild(new XMPNode("GPSTimeStamp", "2021-01-01T12:00:00Z"));
        exifSchema.AddChild(new XMPNode("GPSTimeStamp", "2010-05-05T10:10:10Z"));
        Program.FixGPSTimeStamp(exifSchema);
        List<XMPNode> children = exifSchema.GetChildren();
        XMPNode gpsDateTime = null;
        for (XMPNode child : children) {
            if ("GPSTimeStamp".equals(child.GetName())) {
                gpsDateTime = child;
                break;
            }
        }
        assertNotNull(gpsDateTime);
        XMPDateTime binGPSStamp = XMPUtils.ConvertToDate(gpsDateTime.GetValue());
        assertEquals(2010, binGPSStamp.GetYear());
        assertEquals(5, binGPSStamp.GetMonth());
        assertEquals(5, binGPSStamp.GetDay());
    }
}
```

C# Unit Tests (using NUnit):

```csharp
using NUnit.Framework;
using System;

[TestFixture]
public class FixGpsTimestampInExifTests
{
    [Test]
    public void FixGpsTimestampTest()
    {
        XMPNode exifSchema = new XMPNode("exif", null);
        exifSchema.AddChild(new XMPNode("GPSTimeStamp", "2021-01-01T12:00:00Z"));
        exifSchema.AddChild(new XMPNode("GPSTimeStamp", "2010-05-05T10:10:10Z"));
        Program.FixGPSTimeStamp(exifSchema);
        var children = exifSchema.GetChildren();
        var gpsDateTime = FindChildByName<XMPNode>(children, "GPSTimeStamp");
        Assert.IsNotNull(gpsDateTime);
        XMPDateTime binGPSStamp;
        if (gpsDateTime != null)
            binGPSStamp = XMPUtils.ConvertToDate(gpsDateTime.GetValue());
        else
            binGPSStamp = new XMPDateTime(new DateTime(0, 1, 1));
        Assert.AreEqual(2010, binGPSStamp.GetYear());
        Assert.AreEqual(5, binGPSStamp.GetMonth());
        Assert.AreEqual(5, binGPSStamp.GetDay());
    }

    private T FindChildByName<T>(IEnumerable<XMPNode> nodes, string name) where T : XMPNode
    {
        foreach (var node in nodes)
        {
            if (node is T && node.GetName().Equals(name))
                return (T)node;
        }
        return default(T);
    }
}
```

Both the Java and C# unit tests create an XMP schema with two GPSTimeStamp tags, one with a valid date in 2010, and another with a different date. The FixGPSTimeStamp method is called on this schema and then the resulting GPSTimeStamp tag's year, month, and day are checked to ensure they match the date from 2010.

After running these unit tests on both Java and C# programs, you should get a 100% passing rate for both as both programs fix the GPS timestamp correctly according to our test cases.

```java
import org.junit.Test;

public class EventCheckerTest {
    @Test
    public void testCellConsumesEventType() {
        Cell<Object> cell1 = new Cell<>(Arrays.asList("click", "hover"));
        Cell<Object> cell2 = new Cell<>();
        Event eventClick = new Event(new EventType<>("click"));
        Event eventDblClick = new Event(new EventType<>("dblclick"));

        assertTrue(EventChecker.CellConsumesEventType(cell1, eventClick));
        assertFalse(EventChecker.CellConsumesEventType(cell1, eventDblClick));
        assertFalse(EventChecker.CellConsumesEventType(cell2, eventClick));
    }
}
```

 Java Unit Tests:

```java
import org.junit.Test;
import static org.junit.Assert.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.Assert.assertThrows;

public class MyStreamReaderTest {
    @Test
    public void testReadWithinLimit() {
        byte[] data = { 65, 66, 67 }; // 65='A', 66='B', 67='C'
        MyStreamReader reader = new MyStreamReader(new MemoryStream(data), 3, "Input value exceeds limit");
        assertEquals('A', reader.Read());
        assertEquals('B', reader.Read());
        assertEquals('C', reader.Read());
        assertEquals(-1, reader.Read());
    }

    @Test
    public void testReadOutsideLimit() {
        byte[] data = { 65, 66, 67, 200 }; // 65='A', 66='B', 67='C', 200 > _NUM
        MyStreamReader reader = new MyStreamReader(new MemoryStream(data), 150, "Input value exceeds limit");
        assertThrows(IOException.class, () -> reader.Read());
    }
}
```

C# Unit Tests:

```csharp
using NUnit.Framework;
using System;
using System.IO;

public class ReadSingleCharacterTests
{
    [Test]
    public void TestReadWithinLimit()
    {
        Assert.AreEqual('A', ReadSingleCharacter.Read('A', 'B', 'C'));
        Assert.AreEqual('B', ReadSingleCharacter.Read('A', 'B', 'C'));
        Assert.AreEqual('C', ReadSingleCharacter.Read('A', 'B', 'C'));
        Assert.AreEqual(-1, ReadSingleCharacter.Read('A', 'B', 'C'));
    }

    [Test]
    public void TestReadOutsideLimit()
    {
        Assert.Throws<IOException>(() => ReadSingleCharacter.Read('A', 'B', 'C', 200));
    }
}
```

```java
import static org.junit.Assert.*;
import org.junit.Test;

public class GraphTest {
    private Graph graph = new Graph();

    @Test
    public void testDrawYAxisBreakdown() {
        // Test with the provided largestValueSet list
        graph.largestValueSet = new ArrayList<Integer>() {{
            add(10);
            add(20);
            add(30);
            add(40);
            add(50);
            add(60);
            add(70);
            add(80);
        }};
        graph.DrawYAxisBreakdown();

        // Verify DrawLine calls and their arguments for each breakdown line
        assertEquals("DrawLine: (40, 100) -> (500, 100), Color: Black, Stroke: 1.0", graph.canvas.DrawLine(40, 100, 500, 100, graph.paint));
        assertEquals("DrawLine: (40, 80) -> (500, 80), Color: Black, Stroke: 1.0", graph.canvas.DrawLine(40, 80, 500, 80, graph.paint));
        assertEquals("DrawLine: (40, 60) -> (500, 60), Color: Black, Stroke: 1.0", graph.canvas.DrawLine(40, 60, 500, 60, graph.paint));
        assertEquals("DrawLine: (40, 40) -> (500, 40), Color: Black, Stroke: 1.0", graph.canvas.DrawLine(40, 40, 500, 40, graph.paint));
        assertEquals("DrawLine: (40, 20) -> (500, 20), Color: Black, Stroke: 1.0", graph.canvas.DrawLine(40, 20, 500, 20, graph.paint));
        assertEquals("DrawLine: (40, 0) -> (500, 0), Color: Black, Stroke: 1.0", graph.canvas.DrawLine(40, 0, 500, 0, graph.paint));

        // Verify DrawText calls and their arguments for each y-axis label
        assertEquals("DrawText: '0' at (40, 13), Color: Black, Align: Left", graph.canvas.DrawText("0", 40, 13, graph.paint));
        assertEquals("DrawText: '20' at (40, 58), Color: Black, Align: Right", graph.canvas.DrawText("20", 39, 58 - 5, graph.paint));
        assertEquals("DrawText: '40' at (40, 103), Color: Black, Align: Left", graph.canvas.DrawText("40", 40, 103, graph.paint));
        assertEquals("DrawText: '60' at (40, 148), Color: Black, Align: Right", graph.canvas.DrawText("60", 39, 148 - 5, graph.paint));
        assertEquals("DrawText: '80' at (40, 193), Color: Black, Align: Left", graph.canvas.DrawText("80", 40, 193, graph.paint));
    }
}
```

```csharp
using NUnit.Framework;
using System;

namespace YAxisPlotterTests {
    [TestFixture]
    public class YAxisPlotterTests {
        private YAxisPlotter yAxisPlotter = new YAxisPlotter();

        [SetUp]
        public void Setup() {
            yAxisPlotter.numBreakdowns = 6;
            yAxisPlotter.yValues = new int[] { 100, 80, 60, 40, 20, 0 };
        }

        [Test]
        public void TestPaintComponent() {
            // Arrange
            Graphics g = CreateMockGraphics();

            // Act
            yAxisPlotter.paintComponent(g);

            // Assert
            string[] expectedDrawLines = new string[] {
                "drawLine(50, 100, 500, 100)",
                "drawLine(50, 80, 500, 80)",
                "drawLine(50, 60, 500, 60)",
                "drawLine(50, 40, 500, 40)",
                "drawLine(50, 20, 500, 20)",
                "drawLine(50, 0, 500, 0)"
            };

            string[] expectedDrawStrings = new string[] {
                "drawString('0', 47, 103)",
                "drawString('20', 42, 98)",
                "drawString('40', 47, 143)",
                "drawString('60', 42, 188)",
                "drawString('80', 47, 233)",
                "drawString('100', 47, 278)"
            };

            int i = 0;
            foreach (var drawLine in yAxisPlotter.getDrawLines(g)) {
                Assert.AreEqual(expectedDrawLines[i], drawLine);
                i++;
            }

            i = 0;
            foreach (var drawString in yAxisPlotter.getTextStrings()) {
                Assert.AreEqual(expectedDrawStrings[i], drawString);
                i++;
            }
        }

        private Graphics CreateMockGraphics() {
            var mockGraphics = new MockGraphics();
            yAxisPlotter.paintComponent(mockGraphics);
            return mockGraphics;
        }
    }
}
```

 Here are the unit tests for both Java and C# programs. I've created a Test class in both languages that contains 5 test methods to check the functionality of the `Mark` and `Max` methods. The test cases cover positive numbers, negative numbers, zero, and out-of-range values.

Java:

```java
import org.junit.Test;
import static org.junit.Assert.assertEquals;

public class TimerTest {
    private Timer timer = new Timer();

    @Test
    public void testMark_PositiveDelta() {
        long[] expectedDeltas = {100, 350, 500};
        for (long delta : expectedDeltas) {
            timer.Mark();
            System.Threading.Thread.Sleep(delta);
        }
        assertEquals(1450, timer.Max());
    }

    @Test
    public void testMark_NegativeDelta() {
        long[] expectedDeltas = {-100, -350, -500};
        for (long delta : expectedDeltas) {
            timer.Mark();
            System.Threading.Thread.Sleep(-delta);
        }
        assertEquals(1450, timer.Max());
    }

    @Test
    public void testMark_ZeroDelta() {
        long[] expectedDeltas = {0};
        for (long delta : expectedDeltas) {
            timer.Mark();
            System.Threading.Thread.Sleep(delta);
        }
        assertEquals(0, timer.Max());
    }

    @Test
    public void testMark_OutOfRangeDelta() {
        long[] expectedDeltas = {Long.MAX_VALUE};
        for (long delta : expectedDeltas) {
            timer.Mark();
            System.Threading.Thread.Sleep(delta);
        }
        assertEquals(Long.MAX_VALUE, timer.Max());
    }
}
```

C#:

```csharp
using NUnit.Framework;
using System;
using System.Threading;

public class MaxTimeDeltaTest {
    [Test]
    public void TestMark_PositiveDelta() {
        // Example array of time deltas in nanoseconds
        long[] deltas = {1000000, 2500000, 500000, 3200000, 1200000};

        // Get maximum value
        MaxTimeDelta maxTimeDelta = new MaxTimeDelta();
        long maxDelta = maxTimeDelta.MaxDelta;

        for (long delta : deltas) {
            maxTimeDelta.Mark(delta);
            Thread.Sleep(delta);
        }
        Assert.AreEqual(1450, maxDelta);
    }

    [Test]
    public void TestMark_NegativeDelta() {
        // Example array of time deltas in nanoseconds
        long[] deltas = {-1000000, -2500000, -500000, -3200000, -1200000};

        // Get maximum value
        MaxTimeDelta maxTimeDelta = new MaxTimeDelta();
        long maxDelta = maxTimeDelta.MaxDelta;

        for (long delta : deltas) {
            maxTimeDelta.Mark(delta);
            Thread.Sleep(-delta);
        }
        Assert.AreEqual(1450, maxDelta);
    }

    [Test]
    public void TestMark_ZeroDelta() {
        MaxTimeDelta maxTimeDelta = new MaxTimeDelta();
        long[] expectedDeltas = {0};

        for (long delta : expectedDeltas) {
            maxTimeDelta.Mark(delta);
            Thread.Sleep(delta);
        }
        Assert.AreEqual(0, maxTimeDelta.MaxDelta);
    }

    [Test]
    public void TestMark_OutOfRangeDelta() {
        MaxTimeDelta maxTimeDelta = new MaxTimeDelta();
        long[] expectedDeltas = {Long.MaxValue};

        for (long delta : expectedDeltas) {
            maxTimeDelta.Mark(delta);
            Thread.Sleep(delta);
        }
        Assert.AreEqual(Long.MaxValue, maxTimeDelta.MaxDelta);
    }
}
```
