 Here are the unit tests for both Java and C# programs. I've created a separate class `RuleGroundingTests` in both languages with the same test methods. The test cases cover empty groundings, extending with non-empty groundings, and extending with an empty (failed) grounding.

Java:
```java
import org.junit.Test;
import static org.junit.Assert.*;
import java.util.Arrays;
import java.util.List;

public class RuleGroundingTests {
    @Test
    public void testEmptyGroundings() {
        RuleGrounding grounding1 = new RuleGrounding(new ArrayList<>());
        assertTrue(grounding1.IsFailed());
    }

    @Test
    public void testNonEmptyGroundings() {
        List<String> groundings1 = Arrays.asList("A", "B");
        List<String> groundings2 = Arrays.asList("C", "D");
        RuleGrounding grounding1 = new RuleGrounding(groundings1);
        RuleGrounding grounding2 = new RuleGrounding(groundings2);

        assertFalse(grounding1.IsFailed());
        List<String> alternatives = Arrays.asList("E", "F");
        grounding1.Extend(alternatives);
        assertTrue(Arrays.equals(groundings1.toArray(), grounding1.GetAlternatives().toArray()));
        assertFalse(grounding2.IsFailed());
        grounding2.Extend(grounding1);
        List<String> expected = Arrays.asList("C", "D", "A", "B", "E", "F");
        assertTrue(Arrays.equals(expected.toArray(), grounding2.GetAlternatives().toArray()));
    }

    @Test
    public void testExtendWithFailedGroundings() {
        List<String> groundings1 = Arrays.asList("A", "B");
        RuleGrounding grounding1 = new RuleGrounding(groundings1);
        RuleGrounding failedGrounding = new RuleGrounding(new ArrayList<>());
        grounding1.Extend(failedGrounding);
        assertTrue(grounding1.IsFailed());
    }
}
```
C#:
```csharp
using Microsoft.VisualStudio.TestTools.UnitTesting;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using static RuleGrounding;

[TestClass]
public class RuleGroundingTests {
    [TestMethod]
    public void TestEmptyGroundings() {
        var grounding1 = new RuleGrounding(new List<string>());
        Assert.IsTrue(grounding1.IsFailed());
    }

    [TestMethod]
    public void TestNonEmptyGroundings() {
        var groundings1 = new List<string> { "A", "B" };
        var groundings2 = new List<string> { "C", "D" };
        var grounding1 = new RuleGrounding(groundings1);
        var grounding2 = new RuleGrounding(groundings2);

        Assert.IsFalse(grounding1.IsFailed());
        var alternatives = new List<string> { "E", "F" };
        grounding1.Extend(alternatives);
        CollectionAssert.AreEqual(groundings1, grounding1.GetAlternatives());
        Assert.IsFalse(grounding2.IsFailed());
        grounding2.Extend(grounding1);
        var expected = new List<string> { "C", "D", "A", "B", "E", "F" };
        CollectionAssert.AreEqual(expected, grounding2.GetAlternatives());
    }

    [TestMethod]
    public void TestExtendWithFailedGroundings() {
        var groundings1 = new List<string> { "A", "B" };
        var grounding1 = new RuleGrounding(groundings1);
        var failedGrounding = new RuleGrounding(new List<string>());
        grounding1.Extend(failedGrounding);
        Assert.IsTrue(grounding1.IsFailed());
    }
}
```

```java
    @Test
    public void testExpectMatchedPattern() {
        Pattern pattern = new Pattern("hello");
        MyParser parser = new MyParser("hello world");
        String result = null;
        try {
            result = parser.Expect(pattern);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            fail("Unexpected exception: " + e.getMessage());
        }
        assertEquals("hello", result);
    }

    @Test
    public void testExpectNotMatchedPattern() {

        MyParser parser = new MyParser("hello world");
        String result = null;

            result = parser.Expect(pattern);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            assertEquals("Pattern not matched: bye hello world", e.getMessage());
        }
```

```csharp
using NUnit.Framework;
using System.IO;
using System.Text.RegularExpressions;

[TestFixture]
public class PatternMatcherTests {
    [Test]
    public void TestMatchMatchedPattern() {
        var pattern = new Regex(@"\d+"); // Example: pattern for digits
        var input = new StringReader("1234567890");
        string matched;
        try {
            matched = PatternMatcher.matchPatternFromInputStream(pattern, input);
            Assert.AreEqual("1", matched); // Assuming the first digit is returned as a result
        } catch (Exception e) {
            Assert.Fail($"Unexpected exception: {e.Message}");
        }
    }

    [Test]
    public void TestMatchNotMatchedPattern() {
        var pattern = new Regex(@"[a-z]+"); // Example: pattern for letters
        var input = new StringReader("1234567890");
        string matched;
        try {
            matched = PatternMatcher.matchPatternFromInputStream(pattern, input);
            Assert.AreEqual(null, matched);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            Assert.AreEqual("Pattern not found in input stream", e.Message);
        }
    }
}
```

 For the given Java and C# code, I will generate the same unit tests for both programs. The unit tests are designed to verify that the `getTableTypes()` method returns the table types in a sorted order.

Java Unit Test:

```java
import org.junit.Test;
import static org.junit.Assert.*;

public class DatabaseMetaDataTest {
    private Connection con = new Connection();

    @Test
    public void testGetTableTypesOrder() throws Exception {
        DatabaseMetaData dmd = con.getMetaData();
        ResultSet rs = dmd.getTableTypes();
        String previousType = "";
        while (rs.next()) {
            String type = rs.getString(1);
            assertTrue(String.compare(type, previousType) >= 0);
            previousType = type;
        }
    }
}
```

C# Unit Test:

```csharp
using NUnit.Framework;
using System;
using System.Data;
using System.Data.SQLite;

public class DatabaseMetaDataTest
{
    private Connection con = new Connection();

    [Test]
    public void TestGetTableTypesOrder()
    {
        DatabaseMetaData dmd = con.GetMetaData();
        var rs = dmd.GetTableTypes();
        string previousType = string.Empty;
        while (rs.Read())
        {
            string type = (string)rs[1]; // Assuming column 1 corresponds to index 0, as that's the typical index for the first column in DataTables.
            Assert.IsTrue(string.Compare(type, previousType) >= 0);
            previousType = type;
        }
    }
}
```

CODE COUNT: 98

 Here are the unit tests for both the Java and C# code. I've created 5 test cases that cover different scenarios, such as cache hit, cache miss, multiple cache hits, cache invalidation, and exception handling. The tests are designed to check if the same FileInputStream instance is returned from cache for the same file path and also to verify the correct behavior when an exception occurs.

Java:

```java
import org.junit.Test;
import static org.junit.Assert.*;
import java.io.*;
import java.util.*;

public class FileCacheTests {
    private final Program program = new Program();

    @Test
    public void testCacheHit() throws IOException {
        String tempFilePath1 = Path.getTempFileName();
        File.write(new File(tempFilePath1), "Sample content for cache test.".getBytes());
        FileStream fs1 = program.UpdateCache(tempFilePath1);
        FileStream fs2 = program.UpdateCache(tempFilePath1);
        assertSame(fs1, fs2);
    }

    @Test
    public void testCacheMiss() throws IOException {
        String tempFilePath1 = Path.getTempFileName();
        File.write(new File(tempFilePath1), "Sample content for cache test.".getBytes());
        FileStream fs1 = program.UpdateCache(tempFilePath1);
        FileStream fs2 = program.UpdateCache(Path.getTempFileName());
        assertNotSame(fs1, fs2);
    }

    @Test
    public void testMultipleCacheHits() throws IOException {
        String tempFilePath1 = Path.getTempFileName();
        String tempFilePath2 = Path.getTempFileName();
        File.write(new File(tempFilePath1), "Sample content for cache test.".getBytes());
        FileStream fs1 = program.UpdateCache(tempFilePath1);
        FileStream fs2 = program.UpdateCache(tempFilePath2);
        FileStream fs3 = program.UpdateCache(tempFilePath1);
        assertSame(fs1, fs3);
        assertNotSame(fs1, fs2);
    }

    @Test(expected = IOException.class)
    public void testException() throws IOException {
        String tempFilePath1 = Path.getTempFileName();
        program.UpdateCache(tempFilePath1 + "non_existent");
    }

    @Test
    public void testCacheInvalidation() throws IOException {
        String tempFilePath1 = Path.getTempFileName();
        FileStream fs1 = program.UpdateCache(tempFilePath1);
        program.Main(new String[]{});
        assertNull(program.fileNameAndStreamCache.get(tempFilePath1));
    }
}
```

C#:

```csharp
using System;
using NUnit.Framework;
using static System.IO.File;

public class FileCacheTests
{
    private readonly FileCache fileCache = new FileCache();

    [Test]
    public void TestCacheHit()
    {
        string file1 = Path.GetTempFileName();
        WriteAllText(file1, "Sample content for cache test.");
        using (var fis1 = fileCache.GetFileStream(file1))
        using (var fis2 = fileCache.GetFileStream(file1))
        {
            Assert.IsTrue(object.ReferenceEquals(fis1, fis2));
        }
    }

    [Test]
    public void TestCacheMiss()
    {
        string file1 = Path.GetTempFileName();
        WriteAllText(file1, "Sample content for cache test.");
        using (var fis1 = fileCache.GetFileStream(file1))
        using (var fis2 = fileCache.GetFileStream(Path.GetTempFileName()))
        {
            Assert.IsFalse(object.ReferenceEquals(fis1, fis2));
        }
    }

    [Test]
    public void TestMultipleCacheHits()
    {
        string file1 = Path.GetTempFileName();
        string file2 = Path.GetTempFileName();
        WriteAllText(file1, "Sample content for cache test.");
        using (var fis1 = fileCache.GetFileStream(file1))
        using (var fis2 = fileCache.GetFileStream(file2))
        using (var fis3 = fileCache.GetFileStream(file1))
        {
            Assert.IsTrue(object.ReferenceEquals(fis1, fis3));
            Assert.IsFalse(object.ReferenceEquals(fis1, fis2));
        }
    }

    [Test]
    public void TestException()
    {
        string file1 = Path.GetTempFileName() + "non_existent";
        Assert.Throws<IOException>(() => fileCache.GetFileStream(file1));
    }

    [Test]
    public void TestCacheInvalidation()
    {
        string file1 = Path.GetTempFileName();
        using (var fis1 = fileCache.GetFileStream(file1))
        {
            fileCache.CloseAll();
            Assert.Throws<IOException>(() => fileCache.GetFileStream(file1));
        }
    }
}
```

 For both Java and C# codes, I will generate the same unit tests that focus on testing the `RemoveSwipeListener` and `PrintListeners` methods in Java (SwipeManager class) and the `removeListener` method and `notifyListeners` method in C# (ListenerManager class).

Java Unit Tests:
```java
import org.junit.Test;
import static org.junit.Assert.assertEquals;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

public class SwipeManagerTest {
    private SwipeManager manager;
    private List<SwipeListener> listeners = new ArrayList<>();

    @org.junit.Before
    public void setUp() {
        manager = new SwipeManager();
        listeners.add(new SwipeListener());
        listeners.add(new SwipeListener());
        for (SwipeListener listener : listeners) {
            manager.AddSwipeListener(listener);
        }
    }

    @Test
    public void testRemoveSwipeListener() {
        manager.RemoveSwipeListener(listeners.get(0));
        assertEquals(1, manager.mListeners.Count);
    }

    @Test
    public void testPrintListenersAfterAddingListeners() {
        manager = new SwipeManager();
        listeners.add(new SwipeListener());
        manager.AddSwipeListener(listeners.get(0));
        manager.PrintListeners();
        assertEquals("Current listeners count: 1", manager.PrintListeners().trim());
    }

    @Test
    public void testPrintListenersAfterRemovingListeners() {
        SwipeManager manager = new SwipeManager();
        SwipeListener listener0 = new SwipeListener();
        SwipeListener listener1 = new SwipeListener();
        manager.AddSwipeListener(listener0);
        manager.AddSwipeListener(listener1);
        manager.PrintListeners();
        manager.RemoveSwipeListener(listener0);
        manager.PrintListeners();
        assertEquals("Current listeners count: 1", manager.PrintListeners().trim());
    }

    @Test
    public void testAddAndRemoveSameListener() {
        SwipeManager manager = new SwipeManager();
        SwipeListener listener = new SwipeListener();
        manager.AddSwipeListener(listener);
        manager.PrintListeners();
        manager.RemoveSwipeListener(listener);
        manager.PrintListeners();
        assertEquals("Current listeners count: 0", manager.PrintListeners().trim());
    }
}
```

C# Unit Tests:
```csharp
using Microsoft.VisualStudio.TestTools.UnitTesting;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;

[TestClass]
public class ListenerManagerTests
{
    private ListenerManager manager;
    private List<Listener> listeners = new List<Listener>();

    [TestInitialize]
    public void SetUp()
    {
        manager = new ListenerManager();
        listeners.Add(new MyListener("Listener1"));
        listeners.Add(new MyListener("Listener2"));
        for (Listener listener : listeners)
        {
            manager.addListener(listener);
        }
    }

    [TestMethod]
    public void TestRemoveListener()
    {
        manager.removeListener(listeners[0]);
        Assert.AreEqual(1, manager.listeners.Count);
    }

    [TestMethod]
    public void TestPrintListenersAfterAddingListeners()
    {
        ListenerManager manager = new ListenerManager();
        MyListener listener = new MyListener("Listener");
        manager.addListener(listener);
        string output = manager.notifyListeners("Event 1").ToString().TrimEnd();
        Assert.AreEqual("Notifying listeners...Listener1 received: Event 1 Listener2 received: Event 1", output);
    }

    [TestMethod]
    public void TestPrintListenersAfterRemovingListeners()
    {
        string output1 = manager.notifyListeners("Event 1").ToString().TrimEnd();
        Assert.AreEqual("Notifying listeners...Listener1 received: Event 1 Listener2 received: Event 1", output1);
        manager.removeListener(listeners[0]);
        string output2 = manager.notifyListeners("Event 2").ToString().TrimEnd();
        Assert.AreEqual("Notifying listeners...Listener2 received: Event 2", output2);
    }

    [TestMethod]
    public void TestAddAndRemoveSameListener()
    {
        ListenerManager manager = new ListenerManager();
        MyListener listener = new MyListener("Listener");
        manager.addListener(listener);
        manager.notifyListeners("Event 1").ToString().TrimEnd();
        manager.removeListener(listener);
        manager.notifyListeners("Event 2").ToString().TrimEnd();
        Assert.AreEqual("Notifying listeners...", manager.notifyListeners("Event 2").ToString().TrimEnd());
    }
}
```
